id="infoSection"><h2>Status:</h2><p class="intro">Point in time view as at 15/10/2026. </p></div><div id="statusWarning" class="uptoDate"><div class="title"><h2>Changes to legislation:</h2><p class="intro">There are currently no known outstanding effects for The Global Human Rights and International Humanitarian Law Sanctions Regulations 2026.<a href="#Scenario1Help" class="helpItem helpItemToBot"><img src="/images/chrome/helpIcon.gif" alt=" Help about Changes to Legislation" /></a></p><div class="help" id="Scenario1Help"><span class="icon"></span><div class="content"><a href="#" class="close"><img alt="Close" src="/images/chrome/closeIcon.gif" /></a><h3>Changes to Legislation</h3><p>Revised legislation carried on this site may not be fully up to date. At the current time any known changes or effects made by subsequent legislation have been applied to the text of the legislation you are viewing by the editorial team. Please see ‘Frequently Asked Questions’ for details regarding the timescales for which new effects are identified and recorded on this site.</p></div></div></div></div><div id="viewLegContents" xml:lang="en"><div class="LegSnippet" id="viewLegSnippet"><div class="LegClearFix LegPrelims"> <p class="LegDraftText">Regulations made by the Secretary of State, laid before Parliament under section 55(3) of the Sanctions and Anti-Money Laundering Act <a class="LegCitation" title="Go to item of legislation" rel="cite" href="/id/ukpga/2018/13">2018 (c. 13)</a>, for approval by resolution of each House of Parliament within 28 days beginning with the day on which the instrument was made, subject to extension for periods of dissolution, prorogation or adjournment of both Houses for more than four days.&#xD;</p> <p class="LegBanner">Statutory Instruments</p><h1 class="LegNo">2026 No. 1071</h1><p class="LegSubject">SANCTIONS</p><h1 class="LegTitle">The Global Human Rights and International Humanitarian Law Sanctions Regulations 2026</h1><div class="LegDate"><p class="LegDateText">Made</p><p class="LegDateDate">12th October 2026</p></div><div class="LegDate"><p class="LegDateText">Laid before Parliament</p><p class="LegDateDate">13th October 2026</p></div><div class="LegDate"><p class="LegDateText">Coming into force</p><p class="LegDateDate">15th October 2026</p></div><a class="LegAnchorID" id="Legislation-Preamble"></a><div class="LegEnactingText"><p class="LegText">The Secretary of State(<a class="LegFootnoteRef" href="#f00001" title="Go to footnote 1" id="Backf00001">1</a>) makes these Regulations in exercise of the powers conferred by section 1(1)(c) and (3)(b), 3(1)(a) and (d)(i), 3A, 4, 9(2)(a), 10(2) to (4), 11(1A) to (9), 12(1A) to (9), 15(2)(a) and (b), (3), (3A), (3B), (4)(b), (5) and (6), 16, 17(2) to (5) and (8), 21(1), 45(1)(a), 54(1) and (2), and 62(4) and (5) of the Sanctions and Anti-Money Laundering Act 2018(<a class="LegFootnoteRef" href="#f00002" title="Go to footnote 2" id="Backf00002">2</a>).&#xD;</p></div></div><div class="LegAnnotations"><p class="LegAnnotationsGroupHeading">Modifications etc. (not altering text)</p><div class="LegCommentaryItem" id="commentary-key-61094fbce5326d44a25892fcf14bba16"><p class="LegCommentaryPara"><span class="LegCommentaryType">C1</span><span class="LegCommentaryText">Regulations extended (British territories) (with modifications) (15.10.2026) by <a class="LegCitation" title="The Global Human Rights and International Humanitarian Law Sanctions (Overseas Territories) Order 2026" rel="cite" href="/id/uksi/2026/1089">The Global Human Rights and International Humanitarian Law Sanctions (Overseas Territories) Order 2026 (S.I. 2026/1089)</a>, <a class="LegCitation" href="/id/uksi/2026/1089/regulation/1/2" title="Go to The Global Human Rights and International Humanitarian Law Sanctions (Overseas Territories) Order 2026 regs. 1(2)" rel="cite">regs. 1(2)</a>, <a class="LegCitation" href="/id/uksi/2026/1089/regulation/2" title="Go to The Global Human Rights and International Humanitarian Law Sanctions (Overseas Territories) Order 2026 2" rel="cite">2</a>, <a class="LegCitation" href="/id/uksi/2026/1089/schedule/1" title="Go to The Global Human Rights and International Humanitarian Law Sanctions (Overseas Territories) Order 2026 Schs. 1" rel="cite">Schs. 1</a>, <a class="LegCitation" href="/id/uksi/2026/1089/schedule/2" title="Go to The Global Human Rights and International Humanitarian Law Sanctions (Overseas Territories) Order 2026 2" rel="cite"><strong>2</strong></a></span></p></div></div><a class="LegAnchorID" id="part-1"></a><div class="LegClearPart"></div><h2 class="LegPartFirst"><span class="LegPartNo">Part 1<span class="LegExtentRestriction" title="Applies to "><span class="btr"></span>U.K.<span class="bbl"></span><span class="bbr"></span></span></span><span class="LegPartTitle">General</span></h2><h3 class="LegP1GroupTitleFirst">Citation, commencement and extent<span class="LegExtentRestriction" title="Applies to "><span class="btr"></span>U.K.<span class="bbl"></span><span class="bbr"></span></span></h3><p class="LegP1ParaText"><span class="LegP1No" id="regulation-1">1.</span>—(1) These Regulations may be cited as the Global Human Rights and International Humanitarian Law Sanctions Regulations 2026.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(2) These Regulations come into force on 15th October 2026.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(3) These Regulations extend to England and Wales, Scotland and Northern Ireland.&#xD;</p><div class="LegAnnotations"><p class="LegAnnotationsGroupHeading">Commencement Information</p><div class="LegCommentaryItem" id="commentary-key-30f58e8fa85c8a4acb5610a1a2b99e25"><p class="LegCommentaryPara"><span class="LegCommentaryType">I1</span><span class="LegCommentaryText">Reg. 1 in force at 15.10.2026, see <a class="LegCitation" href="/id/uksi/2026/1071/regulation/1/2" title="Go to reg. 1(2)" rel="cite"><strong>reg. 1(2)</strong></a></span></p></div></div><h3 class="LegP1GroupTitle">Interpretation<span class="LegExtentRestriction" title="Applies to "><span class="btr"></span>U.K.<span class="bbl"></span><span class="bbr"></span></span></h3><p class="LegP1ParaText"><span class="LegP1No" id="regulation-2">2.</span>  In these Regulations—&#xD;</p><a class="LegAnchorID" id="p00111"></a><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3">“<span class="LegTerm" id="term-the-act">the Act</span>” means the Sanctions and Anti-Money Laundering Act 2018;&#xD;</p><a class="LegAnchorID" id="p00115"></a><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3">“<span class="LegTerm" id="term-arrangement">arrangement</span>” includes any agreement, understanding, scheme, transaction or series of transactions, whether or not legally enforceable (but see <a href="#schedule-1-paragraph-12" title="Go to Sch. 1 para. 12">paragraph 12</a> of <a href="#schedule-1" title="Go to Sch. 1">Schedule 1</a> for the meaning of that term in that Schedule);&#xD;</p><a class="LegAnchorID" id="p00121"></a><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3">“<span class="LegTerm" id="term-conduct">conduct</span>” includes acts and omissions;&#xD;</p><a class="LegAnchorID" id="p00125"></a><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3">“<span class="LegTerm" id="term-director-disqualification-licence">director disqualification licence</span>” means a licence under <a href="#regulation-27" title="Go to reg. 27">regulation 27</a> (director disqualification licences);&#xD;</p><a class="LegAnchorID" id="p00130"></a><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3">“<span class="LegTerm" id="term-document">document</span>” includes information recorded in any form and, in relation to information recorded otherwise than in legible form, references to its production include producing a copy of the information in legible form;&#xD;</p><a class="LegAnchorID" id="p00134"></a><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3">“<span class="LegTerm" id="term-treasury-licence">Treasury licence</span>” means a licence under <a href="#regulation-26" title="Go to reg. 26">regulation 26</a><a href="#regulation-25-1" title="Go to reg. 25(1)">(1)</a> (Treasury licences);&#xD;</p><a class="LegAnchorID" id="p00140"></a><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3">“<span class="LegTerm" id="term-united-kingdom-person">United Kingdom person</span>” has the same meaning as in section 21 (extra-territorial application) of the Act.&#xD;</p><div class="LegAnnotations"><p class="LegAnnotationsGroupHeading">Commencement Information</p><div class="LegCommentaryItem" id="commentary-key-6c68d539158a11cbdf1ca5718cd538b5"><p class="LegCommentaryPara"><span class="LegCommentaryType">I2</span><span class="LegCommentaryText">Reg. 2 in force at 15.10.2026, see <a class="LegCitation" href="/id/uksi/2026/1071/regulation/1/2" title="Go to reg. 1(2)" rel="cite"><strong>reg. 1(2)</strong></a></span></p></div></div><h3 class="LegP1GroupTitle">Application of prohibitions and requirements outside the United Kingdom<span class="LegExtentRestriction" title="Applies to "><span class="btr"></span>U.K.<span class="bbl"></span><span class="bbr"></span></span></h3><p class="LegP1ParaText"><span class="LegP1No" id="regulation-3">3.</span>—(1) A United Kingdom person may contravene a relevant prohibition by conduct wholly or partly outside the United Kingdom.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(2) Any person may contravene a relevant prohibition by conduct in the territorial sea.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(3) In <a href="#regulation-3" title="Go to reg. 3">this regulation</a>, a “<span class="LegTerm" id="term-relevant-prohibition">relevant prohibition</span>” means any prohibition imposed by—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-3-3-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text"><a href="#regulation-12" title="Go to reg. 12">regulation 12</a><a href="#regulation-12-2" title="Go to reg. 12(2)">(2)</a> (confidential information),</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-3-3-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text"><a href="#part-3" title="Go to Pt. 3">Part 3</a> (Finance), or</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-3-3-c">(c)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">a condition of a Treasury licence.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(4) A United Kingdom person may comply, or fail to comply, with a relevant requirement by conduct wholly or partly outside the United Kingdom.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(5) Any person may comply, or fail to comply, with a relevant requirement by conduct in the territorial sea.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(6) In <a href="#regulation-3" title="Go to reg. 3">this regulation</a>, a “<span class="LegTerm" id="term-relevant-requirement">relevant requirement</span>” means any requirement imposed—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-3-6-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">by or under <a href="#part-7" title="Go to Pt. 7">Part 7</a> (Information and records), or by reason of a request made under a power conferred by that Part, or</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-3-6-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">by a condition of a Treasury licence.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(7) Nothing in <a href="#regulation-3" title="Go to reg. 3">this regulation</a> is to be taken to prevent a relevant prohibition or a relevant requirement from applying to conduct (by any person) in the United Kingdom.&#xD;</p><div class="LegAnnotations"><p class="LegAnnotationsGroupHeading">Commencement Information</p><div class="LegCommentaryItem" id="commentary-key-6e2dad3d65a047f9d2255da4c98674a0"><p class="LegCommentaryPara"><span class="LegCommentaryType">I3</span><span class="LegCommentaryText">Reg. 3 in force at 15.10.2026, see <a class="LegCitation" href="/id/uksi/2026/1071/regulation/1/2" title="Go to reg. 1(2)" rel="cite"><strong>reg. 1(2)</strong></a></span></p></div></div><h3 class="LegP1GroupTitle">Purposes<span class="LegExtentRestriction" title="Applies to "><span class="btr"></span>U.K.<span class="bbl"></span><span class="bbr"></span></span></h3><p class="LegP1ParaText"><span class="LegP1No" id="regulation-4">4.</span>—(1) The purposes of the regulations contained in this instrument are to deter, and provide accountability for, activities falling within paragraphs (2) to (4).&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(2) An activity falls within <a href="#regulation-4-2" title="Go to reg. 4(2)">this paragraph</a> if it is an activity which, if carried out by or on behalf of a State within the territory of that State, would amount to a serious violation by that State of an individual's—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-4-2-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">right to life,</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-4-2-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">right not to be subjected to torture or cruel, inhuman or degrading treatment or punishment,</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-4-2-c">(c)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">right to be free from slavery, not to be held in servitude or required to perform forced or compulsory labour, or</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-4-2-d">(d)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">right to liberty and security of person,</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2Text">whether or not the activity is carried out by or on behalf of a State.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(3) An activity falls within <a href="#regulation-4-3" title="Go to reg. 4(3)">this paragraph</a> if it constitutes a serious violation of international humanitarian law.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(4) An activity falls within this paragraph if it constitutes obstruction of the delivery or distribution of, or access to, humanitarian assistance.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(5) An activity falls within <a href="#regulation-4-2" title="Go to reg. 4(2)">paragraph (2)</a>, <a href="#regulation-4-3" title="Go to reg. 4(3)">(3)</a> or <a href="#regulation-4-4" title="Go to reg. 4(4)">(4)</a> if it is carried out—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-4-5-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">outside the British Islands and British overseas territories by any person, or</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-4-5-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">in the British Islands or a British overseas territory by a person who is not a United Kingdom national or a person incorporated or constituted under the law of any part of the British Islands or a British overseas territory.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(6) In paragraphs (2) to (4) an “<span class="LegTerm" id="term-activity">activity</span>” includes an omission.&#xD;</p><div class="LegAnnotations"><p class="LegAnnotationsGroupHeading">Commencement Information</p><div class="LegCommentaryItem" id="commentary-key-10359b80678e8d11eec1ca1e7c3a41dd"><p class="LegCommentaryPara"><span class="LegCommentaryType">I4</span><span class="LegCommentaryText">Reg. 4 in force at 15.10.2026, see <a class="LegCitation" href="/id/uksi/2026/1071/regulation/1/2" title="Go to reg. 1(2)" rel="cite"><strong>reg. 1(2)</strong></a></span></p></div></div><a class="LegAnchorID" id="part-2"></a><div class="LegClearPart"></div><h2 class="LegPart"><span class="LegPartNo">Part 2<span class="LegExtentRestriction" title="Applies to "><span class="btr"></span>U.K.<span class="bbl"></span><span class="bbr"></span></span></span><span class="LegPartTitle">Designation of persons</span></h2><h3 class="LegP1GroupTitleFirst">Power to designate persons<span class="LegExtentRestriction" title="Applies to "><span class="btr"></span>U.K.<span class="bbl"></span><span class="bbr"></span></span></h3><p class="LegP1ParaText"><span class="LegP1No" id="regulation-5">5.</span>—(1) The Secretary of State may designate persons by name for the purposes of any of the following—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-5-1-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text"><a href="#regulation-14" title="Go to reg. 14">regulations 14</a> to <a href="#regulation-18" title="Go to reg. 18">18</a> (asset-freeze etc.);</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-5-1-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text"><a href="#regulation-20" title="Go to reg. 20">regulation 20</a> (director disqualification sanctions);</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-5-1-c">(c)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text"><a href="#regulation-21" title="Go to reg. 21">regulation 21</a> (immigration).</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(2) The Secretary of State may designate persons by description for the purposes of any of the following—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-5-2-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text"><a href="#regulation-14" title="Go to reg. 14">regulations 14</a> to <a href="#regulation-18" title="Go to reg. 18">18</a>;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-5-2-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text"><a href="#regulation-20" title="Go to reg. 20">regulation 20</a>;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-5-2-c">(c)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text"><a href="#regulation-21" title="Go to reg. 21">regulation 21</a>.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(3) The Secretary of State may designate different persons for the purposes of different provisions mentioned in <a href="#regulation-5-1" title="Go to reg. 5(1)">paragraph (1)</a> or <a href="#regulation-5-2" title="Go to reg. 5(2)">(2)</a>.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(4) For the purposes of these Regulations, persons “designated under <a href="#regulation-5" title="Go to reg. 5">regulation 5</a>” for the purpose of a particular regulation means—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-5-4-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">persons who are designated by name under <a href="#regulation-5-1" title="Go to reg. 5(1)">paragraph (1)</a> for the purposes of that particular regulation, and</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-5-4-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">where the Secretary of State makes provision under <a href="#regulation-5-2" title="Go to reg. 5(2)">paragraph (2)</a> that persons of a specified description are designated persons for the purposes of that regulation, persons of that description.</span>&#xD;</p><div class="LegAnnotations"><p class="LegAnnotationsGroupHeading">Commencement Information</p><div class="LegCommentaryItem" id="commentary-key-b737ae84634820fe043dbb07975c41e6"><p class="LegCommentaryPara"><span class="LegCommentaryType">I5</span><span class="LegCommentaryText">Reg. 5 in force at 15.10.2026, see <a class="LegCitation" href="/id/uksi/2026/1071/regulation/1/2" title="Go to reg. 1(2)" rel="cite"><strong>reg. 1(2)</strong></a></span></p></div></div><h3 class="LegP1GroupTitle">Conditions for designation of persons by name<span class="LegExtentRestriction" title="Applies to "><span class="btr"></span>U.K.<span class="bbl"></span><span class="bbr"></span></span></h3><p class="LegP1ParaText"><span class="LegP1No" id="regulation-6">6.</span>—(1) The Secretary of State may choose whether to designate a person under <a href="#regulation-5" title="Go to reg. 5">regulation 5</a><a href="#regulation-5-1" title="Go to reg. 5(1)">(1)</a> (power to designate persons by name) under—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-6-1-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">the standard procedure, or</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-6-1-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">the urgent procedure.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(2) <a href="#regulation-6-3" title="Go to reg. 6(3)">Paragraph (3)</a> applies where the Secretary of State chooses to designate a person under the standard procedure.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(3) The Secretary of State may not designate a person except where condition A is met.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(4) <a href="#regulation-6-5" title="Go to reg. 6(5)">Paragraphs (5)</a> to <a href="#regulation-6-7" title="Go to reg. 6(7)">(7)</a> apply where the Secretary of State chooses to designate a person under the urgent procedure.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(5) The Secretary of State may designate a person where condition A is not met, but conditions B and C are met.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(6) A person ceases to be a designated person at the end of the period of 56 days beginning with the day following the day on which the person became a designated person unless, within that period, the Secretary of State certifies that—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-6-6-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">condition A is met, or</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-6-6-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">conditions B and C continue to be met.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(7) Where the Secretary of State makes a certification under <a href="#regulation-6-6" title="Go to reg. 6(6)">paragraph (6)</a><a href="#regulation-6-6-b" title="Go to reg. 6(6)(b)">(b)</a>, the designation ceases to have effect at the end of the period of 56 days beginning with the day immediately following the period mentioned in <a href="#regulation-6-6" title="Go to reg. 6(6)">paragraph (6)</a> unless, within that period, the Secretary of State certifies that condition A is met.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(8) Condition A is that the Secretary of State has reasonable grounds to suspect that that person is an involved person.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(9) Condition B is that relevant provision (whenever made) applies to, or in relation to, the person under the law of—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-6-9-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">the United States of America;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-6-9-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">the European Union;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-6-9-c">(c)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">Australia;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-6-9-d">(d)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">Canada.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(10) Condition C is that the Secretary of State considers that it is in the public interest to make designations under the urgent procedure.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(11) For the purposes of condition B, “relevant provision” is provision that the Secretary of State considers—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-6-11-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">corresponds, or is similar, to the type of sanction or sanctions in these Regulations, or</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-6-11-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">is made for purposes corresponding, or similar, to any purpose of any type of sanction or sanctions in these Regulations.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(12) In this regulation, “<span class="LegTerm" id="term-involved-person">involved person</span>” has the meaning given in <a href="#regulation-8" title="Go to reg. 8">regulation 8</a> (designation criteria: meaning of “involved person”).&#xD;</p><div class="LegAnnotations"><p class="LegAnnotationsGroupHeading">Commencement Information</p><div class="LegCommentaryItem" id="commentary-key-6e9e25bc65e7d900d7d339ccff5461b0"><p class="LegCommentaryPara"><span class="LegCommentaryType">I6</span><span class="LegCommentaryText">Reg. 6 in force at 15.10.2026, see <a class="LegCitation" href="/id/uksi/2026/1071/regulation/1/2" title="Go to reg. 1(2)" rel="cite"><strong>reg. 1(2)</strong></a></span></p></div></div><h3 class="LegP1GroupTitle">Conditions for designation of persons by description<span class="LegExtentRestriction" title="Applies to "><span class="btr"></span>U.K.<span class="bbl"></span><span class="bbr"></span></span></h3><p class="LegP1ParaText"><span class="LegP1No" id="regulation-7">7.</span>—(1) The Secretary of State may choose whether to designate persons under regulation 5(2) (power to designate persons by description) under—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-7-1-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">the standard procedure, or</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-7-1-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">the urgent procedure.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(2) <a href="#regulation-7-3" title="Go to reg. 7(3)">Paragraph (3)</a> applies where the Secretary of State chooses to designate persons under the standard procedure.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(3) The Secretary of State may not designate persons except where conditions A and C are met.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(4)  <a href="#regulation-7-5" title="Go to reg. 7(5)">Paragraphs (5)</a> to <a href="#regulation-7-7" title="Go to reg. 7(7)">(7)</a> apply where the Secretary of State chooses to designate persons under the urgent procedure.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(5) The Secretary of State may designate persons where condition C is not met, but conditions A, D and E are met.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(6) Persons of the specified description cease to be designated persons at the end of the period of 56 days beginning with the day following the day on which the Secretary of State provides that persons of that description are designated persons unless, within that period, the Secretary of State certifies that—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-7-6-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">conditions A and C are met, or</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-7-6-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">conditions A, D and E continue to be met.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(7) Where the Secretary of State makes a certification under <a href="#regulation-7-6" title="Go to reg. 7(6)">paragraph (6)</a><a href="#regulation-7-6-b" title="Go to reg. 7(6)(b)">(b)</a>, the designation ceases to have effect at the end of the period of 56 days beginning with the day immediately following the period mentioned in <a href="#regulation-7-6" title="Go to reg. 7(6)">paragraph (6)</a> unless, within that period, the Secretary of State certifies that conditions A and C are met.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(8) Condition A is that the description of persons specified is such that a reasonable person would know whether that person fell within it.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(9) Condition C is that the Secretary of State has reasonable grounds to suspect—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-7-9-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">in a case where the specified description is members of a particular organisation, that the organisation is an involved person, or</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-7-9-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">in the case of any other specified description, that any person falling within that description would necessarily be an involved person.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(10) Condition D is that the description of persons specified is of persons (or some persons) to which, or in relation to which, relevant provision (whenever made) applies under the law of—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-7-10-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">the United States of America;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-7-10-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">the European Union;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-7-10-c">(c)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">Australia;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-7-10-d">(d)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">Canada.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(11) Condition E is that the Secretary of State considers that it is in the public interest to provide that persons of a specified description are designated persons under the urgent procedure.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(12) For the purposes of condition D, “relevant provision” is provision that the Secretary of State considers—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-7-12-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">corresponds, or is similar, to the type of sanction or sanctions in these Regulations, or</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-7-12-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">is made for purposes corresponding, or similar, to any purpose of any type of sanction or sanctions in these Regulations.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(13) In <a href="#regulation-7" title="Go to reg. 7">this regulation</a>—&#xD;</p><a class="LegAnchorID" id="p00544"></a><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3">“<span class="LegTerm" id="term-involved-person">involved person</span>” has the meaning given in <a href="#regulation-8" title="Go to reg. 8">regulation 8</a> (designation criteria: meaning of “involved person”);&#xD;</p><a class="LegAnchorID" id="p00549"></a><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3">“<span class="LegTerm" id="term-organisation">organisation</span>” includes any body, association or combination of persons.&#xD;</p><div class="LegAnnotations"><p class="LegAnnotationsGroupHeading">Commencement Information</p><div class="LegCommentaryItem" id="commentary-key-994980f4e1c5d681fff6ea10a332a421"><p class="LegCommentaryPara"><span class="LegCommentaryType">I7</span><span class="LegCommentaryText">Reg. 7 in force at 15.10.2026, see <a class="LegCitation" href="/id/uksi/2026/1071/regulation/1/2" title="Go to reg. 1(2)" rel="cite"><strong>reg. 1(2)</strong></a></span></p></div></div><h3 class="LegP1GroupTitle">Designation criteria: meaning of “involved person”<span class="LegExtentRestriction" title="Applies to "><span class="btr"></span>U.K.<span class="bbl"></span><span class="bbr"></span></span></h3><p class="LegP1ParaText"><span class="LegP1No" id="regulation-8">8.</span>—(1) For the purposes of <a href="#regulation-6" title="Go to reg. 6">regulations 6</a><a href="#regulation-6-8" title="Go to reg. 6(8)">(8)</a> (conditions for the designation of persons by name) and 7<a href="#regulation-7-9" title="Go to reg. 7(9)">(9)</a> (conditions for the designation of persons by description), an “<span class="LegTerm" id="term-involved-person">involved person</span>” means a person who—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-8-1-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">is or has been involved in a relevant activity,</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-8-1-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">is owned or controlled directly or indirectly (within the meaning of <a href="#regulation-9" title="Go to reg. 9">regulation 9</a> (meaning of “<span class="LegTerm" id="term-owned-or-controlled-directly-or-indirectly">owned or controlled directly or indirectly</span>”)) by a person who is or has been so involved,</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-8-1-c">(c)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">is acting on behalf of or at the direction of a person who is or has been so involved, or</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-8-1-d">(d)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">is a member of, or associated with, a person who is or has been so involved.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(2) In <a href="#regulation-8" title="Go to reg. 8">this regulation</a>, a “<span class="LegTerm" id="term-relevant-activity">relevant activity</span>” means an activity falling within paragraphs <a href="#regulation-4-2" title="Go to reg. 4(2)">(2)</a> to <a href="#regulation-4-4" title="Go to reg. 4(4)">(4)</a> of <a href="#regulation-4" title="Go to reg. 4">regulation 4</a> (purposes).&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(3) For the purposes of this regulation, a person is involved in a relevant activity if—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-8-3-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">the person is responsible for or engages in such an activity;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-8-3-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">the person facilitates, incites, promotes or provides support for such an activity;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-8-3-c">(c)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">the person conceals evidence of such an activity;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-8-3-d">(d)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">the person provides financial services, or makes available funds, economic resources, goods or technology, knowing or having reasonable cause to suspect that those financial services, funds, economic resources, goods or technology will or may contribute to such an activity;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-8-3-e">(e)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">the person provides financial services, or makes available funds, economic resources, goods or technology, to a person mentioned in <a href="#regulation-8-3-a" title="Go to reg. 8(3)(a)">sub-paragraph (a)</a>;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-8-3-f">(f)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">the person profits financially or obtains any other benefit from such an activity;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-8-3-g">(g)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">the person is responsible for the investigation or prosecution of such an activity and intentionally or recklessly fails to fulfil that responsibility;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-8-3-h">(h)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">the person contravenes, or assists with the contravention of, any provision of <a href="#part-3" title="Go to Pt. 3">Part 3</a> (Finance) of these Regulations.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(4) Nothing in any sub-paragraph of <a href="#regulation-8-3" title="Go to reg. 8(3)">paragraph (3)</a> is to be taken to limit the meaning of any of the other sub-paragraphs of <a href="#regulation-8-3-a" title="Go to reg. 8(3)(a)">that paragraph</a>.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(5) Any reference in this regulation to being involved in an activity includes being so involved wherever any actions constituting the involvement take place.&#xD;</p><div class="LegAnnotations"><p class="LegAnnotationsGroupHeading">Commencement Information</p><div class="LegCommentaryItem" id="commentary-key-3abdb0f064c397f6ae52dc50f7ee4f5a"><p class="LegCommentaryPara"><span class="LegCommentaryType">I8</span><span class="LegCommentaryText">Reg. 8 in force at 15.10.2026, see <a class="LegCitation" href="/id/uksi/2026/1071/regulation/1/2" title="Go to reg. 1(2)" rel="cite"><strong>reg. 1(2)</strong></a></span></p></div></div><h3 class="LegP1GroupTitle">Meaning of “owned or controlled directly or indirectly”<span class="LegExtentRestriction" title="Applies to "><span class="btr"></span>U.K.<span class="bbl"></span><span class="bbr"></span></span></h3><p class="LegP1ParaText"><span class="LegP1No" id="regulation-9">9.</span>—(1) A person who is not an individual (“<span class="LegTerm" id="term-c">C</span>”) is “owned or controlled directly or indirectly” by another person (“<span class="LegTerm" id="term-p">P</span>”) if either of the following two conditions is met (or both are met).&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(2) The first condition is that P—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-9-2-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">holds directly or indirectly more than 50% of the shares in C,</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-9-2-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">holds directly or indirectly more than 50% of the voting rights in C, or</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-9-2-c">(c)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">holds the right directly or indirectly to appoint or remove a majority of the board of directors of C.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(3) <a href="#schedule-1" title="Go to Sch. 1">Schedule 1</a> (rules for interpretation of regulation 9(2)) contains provision applying for the purpose of interpreting <a href="#regulation-9-2" title="Go to reg. 9(2)">paragraph (2)</a>.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(4) The second condition is that it is reasonable, having regard to all the circumstances, to expect that P would (if P chose to) be able, in most cases or in significant respects, by whatever means and whether directly or indirectly, to achieve the result that affairs of C are conducted in accordance with P's wishes.&#xD;</p><div class="LegAnnotations"><p class="LegAnnotationsGroupHeading">Commencement Information</p><div class="LegCommentaryItem" id="commentary-key-78aa42749bd505b0d75595f89607664c"><p class="LegCommentaryPara"><span class="LegCommentaryType">I9</span><span class="LegCommentaryText">Reg. 9 in force at 15.10.2026, see <a class="LegCitation" href="/id/uksi/2026/1071/regulation/1/2" title="Go to reg. 1(2)" rel="cite"><strong>reg. 1(2)</strong></a></span></p></div></div><h3 class="LegP1GroupTitle">Notification and publicity where power to designate by name is used<span class="LegExtentRestriction" title="Applies to "><span class="btr"></span>U.K.<span class="bbl"></span><span class="bbr"></span></span></h3><p class="LegP1ParaText"><span class="LegP1No" id="regulation-10">10.</span>—(1) <a href="#regulation-10-2" title="Go to reg. 10(2)">Paragraph (2)</a> applies where the Secretary of State—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-10-1-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">has made a designation under <a href="#regulation-5" title="Go to reg. 5">regulation 5</a><a href="#regulation-5-1" title="Go to reg. 5(1)">(1)</a> (power to designate persons by name), or</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-10-1-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">has by virtue of section 22 (power to vary or revoke designation made under regulations) of the Act varied or revoked a designation made under that regulation.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(2) The Secretary of State—&#xD;</p><p given under <a href="#regulation-10-2" title="Go to reg. 10(2)">paragraph (2)</a><a href="#regulation-10-2-a" title="Go to reg. 10(2)(a)">(a)</a>—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-10-3-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">where the Secretary of State designates a person under the standard procedure, must include a statement of reasons, or</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-10-3-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">where the Secretary of State designates a person under the urgent procedure, must include a statement—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4No" id="regulation-10-3-b-i">(i)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4Text">that the provision is made under the urgent procedure,</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4No" id="regulation-10-3-b-ii">(ii)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4Text">identifying the relevant provision by reference to which the Secretary of State considers that condition B is met in relation to the person, and</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4No" id="regulation-10-3-b-iii">(iii)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4Text">setting out why the Secretary of State considers that condition C is met.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(4) Where the Secretary of State designates a person under the urgent procedure, the Secretary of State must, after the end of the period mentioned in <a href="#regulation-6-6" title="Go to reg. 6(6)">paragraph (6)</a> of <a href="#regulation-6" title="Go to reg. 6">regulation 6</a> (conditions for the designation of persons by name), or if the Secretary of State has made a certification under <a href="#regulation-10-8" title="Go to reg. 10(8)">paragraph (6)</a><a href="#regulation-10-8-b" title="Go to reg. 10(8)(b)">(b)</a> of that regulation, the period mentioned in <a href="#regulation-10-9" title="Go to reg. 10(9)">paragraph (7)</a> of that designated person—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-10-5-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">in the case of a designation at the end of the period mentioned in <a href="#regulation-6" title="Go to reg. 6">regulation 6</a><a href="#regulation-6-6" title="Go to reg. 6(6)">(6)</a> or <a href="#regulation-6-7" title="Go to reg. 6(7)">(7)</a> (as the case may be).</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(6) Matters that would otherwise be required by <a href="#regulation-10-5" title="Go to reg. 10(5)">paragraph (5)</a> State—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-11-2-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">must without delay take such steps as are must include a statement—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4No" id="regulation-11-3-b-i">(i)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4Text">that the condition E is met.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(4) Where the Secretary of State provides that persons of a specified description are designated persons under the urgent procedure, the
