(conditions for the designation of persons by description), or if the Secretary of State has made a certification under <a href="#regulation-11-6" title="Go to reg. 11(6)">paragraph (6)</a><a href="#regulation-7-6-b" title="Go to reg. 7(6)(b)">(b)</a> of <a href="#regulation-7" title="Go to reg. 7">that regulation</a> the period mentioned in <a href="#regulation-11-7" title="Go to reg. 11(7)">paragraph (7)</a> of that regulation, but otherwise without delay—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-11-4-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">in a case where the persons cease to be designated persons, take such steps as are reasonably practicable to inform persons of the specified description that they have ceased to be designated persons, or</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-11-4-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">in any other case, take such steps as are reasonably practicable to give each person of the specified description a statement of reasons.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(5) In <a href="#regulation-11" title="Go to reg. 11">this regulation</a>, a “<span class="LegTerm" id="term-statement-of-reasons">statement of reasons</span>” means a brief statement of the matters that the Secretary of State knows, or has reasonable cause to suspect, in relation to persons of the specified description—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-11-5-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">in the case of a designation under the standard procedure, which have led the Secretary of State to make the provision designating persons of that description, and</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-11-5-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">in the case of a designation under the urgent procedure, as a result of which the persons do not cease to be designated persons at the end of the period mentioned in <a href="#regulation-7" title="Go to reg. 7">regulation 7</a><a href="#regulation-7-6" title="Go to reg. 7(6)">(6)</a> or <a href="#regulation-7-7" title="Go to reg. 7(7)">(7)</a> (as the case may be).</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(6) Matters that would otherwise be required by <a href="#regulation-11-5" title="Go to reg. 11(5)">paragraph (5)</a> to be included in a statement of reasons may be excluded from it where the Secretary of State considers that they should be excluded—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-11-6-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">in the interests of national security or international relations,</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-11-6-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">for reasons connected with the prevention or detection of serious crime in the United Kingdom or elsewhere, or</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-11-6-c">(c)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">in the interests of justice.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(7) The steps taken under <a href="#regulation-11-2" title="Go to reg. 11(2)">paragraph (2)</a><a href="#regulation-11-2-b" title="Go to reg. 11(2)(b)">(b)</a> must—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-11-7-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">unless one or more of the restricted publicity conditions is met, be steps to publicise generally—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4No" id="regulation-11-7-a-i">(i)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4Text">the designation, variation or revocation, and</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4No" id="regulation-11-7-a-ii">(ii)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4Text">in the case of—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP5Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP5No" id="regulation-11-7-a-ii-aa">(aa)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP5Text">a designation under the standard procedure, the statement of reasons relating to it;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP5Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP5No" id="regulation-11-7-a-ii-bb">(bb)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP5Text">a designation under the urgent procedure, the statement required under <a href="#regulation-11-3" title="Go to reg. 11(3)">paragraph (3)</a><a href="#regulation-11-3-b" title="Go to reg. 11(3)(b)">(b)</a> relating to it;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-11-7-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">if one or more of those conditions is met, be steps to inform only such persons as the Secretary of State considers appropriate of—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4No" id="regulation-11-7-b-i">(i)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4Text">the designation, variation or revocation, and</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4No" id="regulation-11-7-b-ii">(ii)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4Text">in the case of—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP5Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP5No" id="regulation-11-7-b-ii-aa">(aa)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP5Text">a designation under the standard procedure, the contents of the statement of reasons relating to it;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP5Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP5No" id="regulation-11-7-b-ii-bb">(bb)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP5Text">a designation under the urgent procedure, the contents of the statement required under <a href="#regulation-10-3" title="Go to reg. 10(3)">paragraph (3)</a><a href="#regulation-10-3-b" title="Go to reg. 10(3)(b)">(b)</a> relating to it.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(8) The “restricted publicity conditions” are that the Secretary of State considers that disclosure of the designation, variation or revocation should be restricted—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-11-8-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">in the interests of national security or international relations,</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-11-8-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">for reasons connected with the prevention or detection of serious crime in the United Kingdom or elsewhere, or</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-11-8-c">(c)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">in the interests of justice.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(9) <a href="#regulation-11-10" title="Go to reg. 11(10)">Paragraph (10)</a> applies if—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-11-9-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">when a designation is made, one or more of the restricted publicity conditions is met, but</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-11-9-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">at any time when the designation has effect, it becomes the case that none of the restricted publicity conditions is met.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(10) The Secretary of State must—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-11-10-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">take such steps as are reasonably practicable to inform persons of the specified description that none of the restricted publicity conditions is now met, and</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-11-10-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">take steps to publicise generally the designation and the statement of reasons, or the statement required under paragraph (3)(b), relating to it.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(11) In this regulation—&#xD;</p><a class="LegAnchorID" id="p01100"></a><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3">“<span class="LegTerm" id="term-condition-d">condition D</span>” means the condition set out in regulation 7(10);&#xD;</p><a class="LegAnchorID" id="p01104"></a><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3">“<span class="LegTerm" id="term-condition-e">condition E</span>” means the condition set out in regulation 7(11).&#xD;</p><div class="LegAnnotations"><p class="LegAnnotationsGroupHeading">Commencement Information</p><div class="LegCommentaryItem" id="commentary-key-43a79cb5378c0e29b5905f7ff24468e5"><p class="LegCommentaryPara"><span class="LegCommentaryType">I11</span><span class="LegCommentaryText">Reg. 11 in force at 15.10.2026, see <a class="LegCitation" href="/id/uksi/2026/1071/regulation/1/2" title="Go to reg. 1(2)" rel="cite"><strong>reg. 1(2)</strong></a></span></p></div></div><h3 class="LegP1GroupTitle">Confidential information in certain cases where designation power used<span class="LegExtentRestriction" title="Applies to "><span class="btr"></span>U.K.<span class="bbl"></span><span class="bbr"></span></span></h3><p class="LegP1ParaText"><span class="LegP1No" id="regulation-12">12.</span>—(1) Where the Secretary of State in accordance with <a href="#regulation-10" title="Go to reg. 10">regulation 10</a><a href="#regulation-10-7" title="Go to reg. 10(7)">(7)</a><a href="#regulation-10-7-b" title="Go to reg. 10(7)(b)">(b)</a> (notification and publicity where power to designate by name is used) or <a href="#regulation-11" title="Go to reg. 11">regulation 11</a><a href="#regulation-11-7" title="Go to reg. 11(7)">(7)</a><a href="#regulation-11-7-b" title="Go to reg. 11(7)(b)">(b)</a> (notification and publicity where power to designate by description is used) informs only certain persons of a designation, variation or revocation and (in the case of a designation) of the contents of the statement of reasons, or the statement to be given under regulation 10(3)(b) or 11(3)(b), the Secretary of State may specify that any of that information is to be treated as confidential.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(2) A person (“<span class="LegTerm" id="term-p">P</span>”) who—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-12-2-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">is provided with information that is to be treated as confidential in accordance with <a href="#regulation-12-1" title="Go to reg. 12(1)">paragraph (1)</a>, or</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-12-2-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">otherwise obtains such information,</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2Text">must not, subject to <a href="#regulation-12-3" title="Go to reg. 12(3)">paragraph (3)</a>, disclose it if P knows, or has reasonable cause to suspect, that the information is to be treated as confidential.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(3) The prohibition in <a href="#regulation-12-2" title="Go to reg. 12(2)">paragraph (2)</a> does not apply to any disclosure made by P with lawful authority.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(4) For this purpose information is disclosed with lawful authority only if and to the extent that—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-12-4-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">the disclosure is by, or is authorised by, the Secretary of State,</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-12-4-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">the disclosure is by or with the consent of the person who is or was the subject of the designation,</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-12-4-c">(c)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">the disclosure is necessary to give effect to a requirement imposed under or by virtue of these Regulations or any other enactment, or</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-12-4-d">(d)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">the disclosure is required, under rules of court, tribunal rules or a court or tribunal order, for the purposes of legal proceedings of any description.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(5) <a href="#regulation-12" title="Go to reg. 12">This regulation</a> does not prevent the disclosure of information that is already, or has previously been, available to the public from other sources.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(6) A person who contravenes the prohibition in <a href="#regulation-12-2" title="Go to reg. 12(2)">paragraph (2)</a> commits an offence.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(7) The High Court (in Scotland, the Court of Session) may, on the application of—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-12-7-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">the person who is the subject of the information, or</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-12-7-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">the Secretary of State,</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2Text">grant an injunction (in Scotland, an interdict) to prevent a breach of the prohibition in <a href="#regulation-12-2" title="Go to reg. 12(2)">paragraph (2)</a>.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(8) In <a href="#regulation-12-4" title="Go to reg. 12(4)">paragraph (4)</a><a href="#regulation-12-4-c" title="Go to reg. 12(4)(c)">(c)</a>, “<span class="LegTerm" id="term-enactment">enactment</span>” has the meaning given by section 54(6) (regulations: general) of the Act.&#xD;</p><div class="LegAnnotations"><p class="LegAnnotationsGroupHeading">Commencement Information</p><div class="LegCommentaryItem" id="commentary-key-cb52d9f2a576ec89022ef0a4de5480e3"><p class="LegCommentaryPara"><span class="LegCommentaryType">I12</span><span class="LegCommentaryText">Reg. 12 in force at 15.10.2026, see <a class="LegCitation" href="/id/uksi/2026/1071/regulation/1/2" title="Go to reg. 1(2)" rel="cite"><strong>reg. 1(2)</strong></a></span></p></div></div><a class="LegAnchorID" id="part-3"></a><div class="LegClearPart"></div><h2 class="LegPart"><span class="LegPartNo">Part 3<span class="LegExtentRestriction" title="Applies to "><span class="btr"></span>U.K.<span class="bbl"></span><span class="bbr"></span></span></span><span class="LegPartTitle">Finance</span></h2><h3 class="LegP1GroupTitleFirst">Meaning of “designated person” in <a href="#part-3" title="Go to Pt. 3">Part 3</a><span class="LegExtentRestriction" title="Applies to "><span class="btr"></span>U.K.<span class="bbl"></span><span class="bbr"></span></span></h3><p class="LegP1ParaText"><span class="LegP1No" id="regulation-13">13.</span>  In <a href="#part-3" title="Go to Pt. 3">this Part</a>, a “<span class="LegTerm" id="term-designated-person">designated person</span>” means a person who is designated under <a href="#regulation-5" title="Go to reg. 5">regulation 5</a> (power to designate persons) for the purposes of <a href="#regulation-14" title="Go to reg. 14">regulations 14</a> to <a href="#regulation-18" title="Go to reg. 18">18</a> (asset-freeze etc).&#xD;</p><div class="LegAnnotations"><p class="LegAnnotationsGroupHeading">Commencement Information</p><div class="LegCommentaryItem" id="commentary-key-f8d6a4e3a8b297bed346ad8199a65ab7"><p class="LegCommentaryPara"><span class="LegCommentaryType">I13</span><span class="LegCommentaryText">Reg. 13 in force at 15.10.2026, see <a class="LegCitation" href="/id/uksi/2026/1071/regulation/1/2" title="Go to reg. 1(2)" rel="cite"><strong>reg. 1(2)</strong></a></span></p></div></div><h3 class="LegP1GroupTitle">Asset-freeze in relation to designated persons<span class="LegExtentRestriction" title="Applies to "><span class="btr"></span>U.K.<span class="bbl"></span><span class="bbr"></span></span></h3><p class="LegP1ParaText"><span class="LegP1No" id="regulation-14">14.</span>—(1) A person (“<span class="LegTerm" id="term-p">P</span>”) must not deal with funds or economic resources owned, held or controlled by a designated person if P knows, or has reasonable cause to suspect, that P is dealing with such funds or economic resources.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(2) <a href="#regulation-14-1" title="Go to reg. 14(1)">Paragraph (1)</a> is subject to <a href="#part-6" title="Go to Pt. 6">Part 6</a> (Exceptions and licences).&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(3) A person who contravenes the prohibition in <a href="#regulation-14-1" title="Go to reg. 14(1)">paragraph (1)</a> commits an offence.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(4) For the purposes of <a href="#regulation-14-1" title="Go to reg. 14(1)">paragraph (1)</a> a person “deals with” funds if the person—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-14-4-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">uses, alters, moves, transfers or allows access to the funds,</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-14-4-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">deals with the funds in any other way that would result in any change in volume, amount, location, ownership, possession, character or destination, or</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-14-4-c">(c)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">makes any other change, including portfolio management, that would enable use of the funds.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(5) For the purposes of <a href="#regulation-14-1" title="Go to reg. 14(1)">paragraph (1)</a> a person “deals with” economic resources if the person—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-14-5-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">exchanges the economic resources for funds, goods or services, or</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-14-5-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">uses the economic resources in exchange for funds, goods or services (whether by pledging them as security or otherwise).</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(6) The reference in <a href="#regulation-14-1" title="Go to reg. 14(1)">paragraph (1)</a> to funds or economic resources that are “owned, held or controlled” by a person includes, in particular, a reference to—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-14-6-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">funds or economic resources in which the person has any legal or equitable interest, regardless of whether the interest is held jointly with any other person and regardless of whether any other person holds an interest in the funds or economic resources;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-14-6-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">any tangible property (other than real property) or bearer security that is comprised in funds or economic resources and is in the possession of the person.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(7) For the purposes of <a href="#regulation-9-1" title="Go to reg. 9(1)">paragraph (1)</a> funds or economic resources are to be treated as owned, held or controlled by a designated person if they are owned, held or controlled by a person who is owned or controlled directly or indirectly (within the meaning of <a href="#regulation-9" title="Go to reg. 9">regulation 9</a> (meaning of “<span class="LegTerm" id="term-owned-or-controlled-directly-or-indirectly">owned or controlled directly or indirectly</span>”)) by the designated person.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(8) For the avoidance of doubt, the reference in <a href="#regulation-14-1" title="Go to reg. 14(1)">paragraph (1)</a> to a designated person includes P if P is a designated person.&#xD;</p><div class="LegAnnotations"><p class="LegAnnotationsGroupHeading">Commencement Information</p><div class="LegCommentaryItem" id="commentary-key-a01ca945eb009ca8a7beeea5096d1a0c"><p class="LegCommentaryPara"><span class="LegCommentaryType">I14</span><span class="LegCommentaryText">Reg. 14 in force at 15.10.2026, see <a class="LegCitation" href="/id/uksi/2026/1071/regulation/1/2" title="Go to reg. 1(2)" rel="cite"><strong>reg. 1(2)</strong></a></span></p></div></div><h3 class="LegP1GroupTitle">Making funds available to designated persons<span class="LegExtentRestriction" title="Applies to "><span class="btr"></span>U.K.<span class="bbl"></span><span class="bbr"></span></span></h3><p class="LegP1ParaText"><span class="LegP1No" id="regulation-15">15.</span>—(1) A person (“<span class="LegTerm" id="term-p">P</span>”) must not make funds available directly or indirectly to a designated person if P knows, or has reasonable cause to suspect, that P is making the funds so available.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(2) <a href="#regulation-15-1" title="Go to reg. 15(1)">Paragraph (1)</a> is subject to <a href="#part-6" title="Go to Pt. 6">Part 6</a> (Exceptions and licences).&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(3) A person who contravenes the prohibition in <a href="#regulation-15-1" title="Go to reg. 15(1)">paragraph (1)</a> commits an offence.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(4) The reference in <a href="#regulation-9-1" title="Go to reg. 9(1)">paragraph (1)</a> to making funds available indirectly to a designated person includes, in particular, a reference to making them available to a person who is owned or controlled directly or indirectly (within the meaning of <a href="#regulation-9" title="Go to reg. 9">regulation 9</a> (meaning of “<span class="LegTerm" id="term-owned-or-controlled-directly-or-indirectly">owned or controlled directly or indirectly</span>”)) by the designated person.&#xD;</p><div class="LegAnnotations"><p class="LegAnnotationsGroupHeading">Commencement Information</p><div class="LegCommentaryItem" id="commentary-key-b51261f81773b43a178d5137a327ede3"><p class="LegCommentaryPara"><span class="LegCommentaryType">I15</span><span class="LegCommentaryText">Reg. 15 in force at 15.10.2026, see <a class="LegCitation" href="/id/uksi/2026/1071/regulation/1/2" title="Go to reg. 1(2)" rel="cite"><strong>reg. 1(2)</strong></a></span></p></div></div><h3 class="LegP1GroupTitle">Making funds available for benefit of designated persons<span class="LegExtentRestriction" title="Applies to "><span class="btr"></span>U.K.<span class="bbl"></span><span class="bbr"></span></span></h3><p class="LegP1ParaText"><span class="LegP1No" id="regulation-16">16.</span>—(1) A person (“<span class="LegTerm" id="term-p">P</span>”) must not make funds available to any person for the benefit of a designated person if P knows, or has reasonable cause to suspect, that P is making the funds so available.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(2) <a href="#regulation-16-1" title="Go to reg. 16(1)">Paragraph (1)</a> is subject to <a href="#part-6" title="Go to Pt. 6">Part 6</a> (Exceptions and licences).&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(3) A person who contravenes the prohibition in <a href="#regulation-16-1" title="Go to reg. 16(1)">paragraph (1)</a> commits an offence.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(4) The reference in <a href="#regulation-16-1" title="Go to reg. 16(1)">paragraph (1)</a> to making funds available to any person for the benefit of a designated person includes making funds available for the benefit of a person who is owned or controlled directly or indirectly (within the meaning of <a href="#regulation-9" title="Go to reg. 9">regulation 9</a> (meaning of “<span class="LegTerm" id="term-owned-or-controlled-directly-or-indirectly">owned or controlled directly or indirectly</span>”)) by the designated person (and references to designated person in <a href="#regulation-16-5" title="Go to reg. 16(5)">paragraph (5)</a> are to be read accordingly).&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(5) For the purposes of <a href="#regulation-16" title="Go to reg. 16">this regulation</a>—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-16-5-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">funds are made available for the benefit of a designated person only if that person thereby obtains, or is able to obtain, a significant financial benefit, and</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-16-5-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">“<span class="LegTerm" id="term-financial-benefit">financial benefit</span>” includes the discharge (or partial discharge) of a financial obligation for which the designated person is wholly or partly responsible.</span>&#xD;</p><div class="LegAnnotations"><p class="LegAnnotationsGroupHeading">Commencement Information</p><div class="LegCommentaryItem" id="commentary-key-75ffaea77b6f00e622ecae7fc2eb7b68"><p class="LegCommentaryPara"><span class="LegCommentaryType">I16</span><span class="LegCommentaryText">Reg. 16 in force at 15.10.2026, see <a class="LegCitation" href="/id/uksi/2026/1071/regulation/1/2" title="Go to reg. 1(2)" rel="cite"><strong>reg. 1(2)</strong></a></span></p></div></div><h3 class="LegP1GroupTitle">Making economic resources available to designated persons<span class="LegExtentRestriction" title="Applies to "><span class="btr"></span>U.K.<span class="bbl"></span><span class="bbr"></span></span></h3><p class="LegP1ParaText"><span class="LegP1No" id="regulation-17">17.</span>—(1) A person (“<span class="LegTerm" id="term-p">P</span>”) must not make economic resources available directly or indirectly to a designated person if P knows, or has reasonable cause to suspect—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-17-1-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">that P is making the economic resources so available, and</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-17-1-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">that the designated person would be likely to exchange the economic resources for, or use them in exchange for, funds, goods or services.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(2) <a href="#regulation-17-1" title="Go to reg. 17(1)">Paragraph (1)</a> is subject to <a href="#part-6" title="Go to Pt. 6">Part 6</a> (Exceptions and licences).&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(3) A person who contravenes the prohibition in <a href="#regulation-17-1" title="Go to reg. 17(1)">paragraph (1)</a> commits an offence.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(4) The reference in <a href="#regulation-9-1" title="Go to reg. 9(1)">paragraph (1)</a> to making economic resources available indirectly to a designated person includes, in particular, a reference to making them available to a person who is owned or controlled directly or indirectly (within the meaning of <a href="#regulation-9" title="Go to reg. 9">regulation 9</a> (meaning of “<span class="LegTerm" id="term-owned-or-controlled-directly-or-indirectly">owned or controlled directly or indirectly</span>”)) by the designated person.&#xD;</p><div class="LegAnnotations"><p class="LegAnnotationsGroupHeading">Commencement Information</p><div class="LegCommentaryItem" id="commentary-key-8ee0742aef6848f9f8e6e352ae375d6d"><p class="LegCommentaryPara"><span class="LegCommentaryType">I17</span><span class="LegCommentaryText">Reg. 17 in force at 15.10.2026, see <a class="LegCitation" href="/id/uksi/2026/1071/regulation/1/2" title="Go to reg. 1(2)" rel="cite"><strong>reg. 1(2)</strong></a></span></p></div></div><h3 class="LegP1GroupTitle">Making economic resources available for benefit of designated persons<span class="LegExtentRestriction" title="Applies to "><span class="btr"></span>U.K.<span class="bbl"></span><span class="bbr"></span></span></h3><p class="LegP1ParaText"><span class="LegP1No" id="regulation-18">18.</span>—(1) A person (“<span class="LegTerm" id="term-p">P</span>”) must not make economic resources available to any person for the benefit of a designated person if P knows, or has reasonable cause to suspect, that P is making the economic resources so available.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(2) <a href="#regulation-18-1" title="Go to reg. 18(1)">Paragraph (1)</a> is subject to Part 6 (Exceptions and licences).&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(3) A person who contravenes the prohibition in <a href="#regulation-18-1" title="Go to reg. 18(1)">paragraph (1)</a> commits an offence.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(4) The reference in <a href="#regulation-18-1" title="Go to reg. 18(1)">paragraph (1)</a> to making economic resources available to any person for the benefit of a designated person includes making economic resources available for the benefit of a person who is owned or controlled directly or indirectly (within the meaning of <a href="#regulation-9" title="Go to reg. 9">regulation 9</a> (meaning of “<span class="LegTerm" id="term-owned-or-controlled-directly-or-indirectly">owned or controlled directly or indirectly</span>”)) by the designated person (and references to designated person in <a href="#regulation-18-5" title="Go to reg. 18(5)">paragraph (5)</a> are to be read accordingly).&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(5) For the purposes of <a href="#regulation-18-1" title="Go to reg. 18(1)">paragraph (1)</a>—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-18-5-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">economic resources are made available for the benefit of a designated person only if that person thereby obtains, or is able to obtain, a significant financial benefit, and</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-18-5-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">“<span class="LegTerm" id="term-financial-benefit">financial benefit</span>” includes the discharge (or partial discharge) of a financial obligation for which the designated person is wholly or partly responsible.</span>&#xD;</p><div class="LegAnnotations"><p class="LegAnnotationsGroupHeading">Commencement Information</p><div class="LegCommentaryItem" id="commentary-key-ef1697a7eb22c4ee2c2f9da0df1b49df"><p class="LegCommentaryPara"><span class="LegCommentaryType">I18</span><span class="LegCommentaryText">Reg. 18 in force at 15.10.2026, see <a class="LegCitation" href="/id/uksi/2026/1071/regulation/1/2" title="Go to reg. 1(2)" rel="cite"><strong>reg. 1(2)</strong></a></span></p></div></div><h3 class="LegP1GroupTitle">Circumventing etc. prohibitions<span class="LegExtentRestriction" title="Applies to "><span class="btr"></span>U.K.<span class="bbl"></span><span class="bbr"></span></span></h3><p class="LegP1ParaText"><span class="LegP1No" id="regulation-19">19.</span>—(1) A person must not intentionally participate in activities knowing that the object or effect of them is (whether directly or indirectly)—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-19-1-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">to circumvent any of the prohibitions in <a href="#regulation-14" title="Go to reg. 14">regulations 14</a> to <a href="#regulation-18" title="Go to reg. 18">18</a> (asset-freeze etc.), or</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-19-1-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">to enable or facilitate the contravention of any such prohibition.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(2) A person who contravenes the prohibition in <a href="#regulation-19-1" title="Go to reg. 19(1)">paragraph (1)</a> commits an offence.&#xD;</p><div class="LegAnnotations"><p class="LegAnnotationsGroupHeading">Commencement Information</p><div class="LegCommentaryItem" id="commentary-key-1c6778437d9c5089ec2ba5995f5c476e"><p class="LegCommentaryPara"><span class="LegCommentaryType">I19</span><span class="LegCommentaryText">Reg. 19 in force at 15.10.2026, see <a class="LegCitation" href="/id/uksi/2026/1071/regulation/1/2" title="Go to reg. 1(2)" rel="cite"><strong>reg. 1(2)</strong></a></span></p></div></div><a class="LegAnchorID" id="part-4"></a><div class="LegClearPart"></div><h2 class="LegPart"><span class="LegPartNo">Part 4<span class="LegExtentRestriction" title="Applies to "><span class="btr"></span>U.K.<span class="bbl"></span><span class="bbr"></span></span></span><span class="LegPartTitle">Director disqualification sanctions</span></h2><h3 class="LegP1GroupTitleFirst">Director disqualification sanctions<span class="LegExtentRestriction" title="Applies to "><span class="btr"></span>U.K.<span class="bbl"></span><span class="bbr"></span></span></h3><p class="LegP1ParaText"><span class="LegP1No" id="regulation-20">20.</span>  A person who is designated under <a href="#regulation-5" title="Go to reg. 5">regulation 5</a> (power to designate persons) for the purposes of <a href="#regulation-20" title="Go to reg. 20">this regulation</a> is subject to director disqualification sanctions for the purposes of—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-20-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">section 11A (designated persons under sanctions legislation) of the Company Directors Disqualification Act 1986(<a class="LegFootnoteRef" href="#f00003" title="Go to footnote 3" id="Backf00003">3</a>), and</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-20-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">article 15A (designated persons under sanctions legislation) of the Company Directors Disqualification (Northern Ireland) Order 2002(<a class="LegFootnoteRef" href="#f00004" title="Go to footnote 4" id="Backf00004">4</a>).</span>&#xD;</p><div class="LegAnnotations"><p class="LegAnnotationsGroupHeading">Commencement Information</p><div class="LegCommentaryItem" id="commentary-key-d686a1180f7924b03fa427fb8c823d3a"><p class="LegCommentaryPara"><span class="LegCommentaryType">I20</span><span class="LegCommentaryText">Reg. 20 in force at 15.10.2026, see <a class="LegCitation" href="/id/uksi/2026/1071/regulation/1/2" title="Go to reg. 1(2)" rel="cite"><strong>reg. 1(2)</strong></a></span></p></div></div><a class="LegAnchorID" id="part-5"></a><div class="LegClearPart"></div><h2 class="LegPart"><span class="LegPartNo">Part 5<span class="LegExtentRestriction" title="Applies to "><span class="btr"></span>U.K.<span class="bbl"></span><span class="bbr"></span></span></span><span class="LegPartTitle">Immigration</span></h2><h3 class="LegP1GroupTitleFirst">Immigration<span class="LegExtentRestriction" title="Applies to "><span class="btr"></span>U.K.<span class="bbl"></span><span class="bbr"></span></span></h3><p class="LegP1ParaText"><span class="LegP1No" id="regulation-21">21.</span>  A person who is designated under <a href="#regulation-5" title="Go to reg. 5">regulation 5</a> (power to designate persons) for the purposes of <a href="#regulation-21" title="Go to reg. 21">this regulation</a> is an excluded person for the purposes of section 8B (persons excluded from the United Kingdom under certain instruments) of the Immigration Act 1971(<a class="LegFootnoteRef" href="#f00005" title="Go to footnote 5" id="Backf00005">5</a>).&#xD;</p><div class="LegAnnotations"><p class="LegAnnotationsGroupHeading">Commencement Information</p><div class="LegCommentaryItem" id="commentary-key-57ea23320bf83ce29c926baad23bbc9b"><p class="LegCommentaryPara"><span class="LegCommentaryType">I21</span><span class="LegCommentaryText">Reg. 21 in force at 15.10.2026, see <a class="LegCitation" href="/id/uksi/2026/1071/regulation/1/2" title="Go to reg. 1(2)" rel="cite"><strong>reg. 1(2)</strong></a></span></p></div></div><a class="LegAnchorID" id="part-6"></a><div class="LegClearPart"></div><h2 class="LegPart"><span class="LegPartNo">Part 6<span class="LegExtentRestriction" title="Applies to "><span class="btr"></span>U.K.<span class="bbl"></span><span class="bbr"></span></span></span><span class="LegPartTitle">Exceptions and licences</span></h2><h3 class="LegP1GroupTitleFirst">Finance: exceptions from prohibitions<span class="LegExtentRestriction" title="Applies to "><span class="btr"></span>U.K.<span class="bbl"></span><span class="bbr"></span></span></h3><p class="LegP1ParaText"><span class="LegP1No" id="regulation-22">22.</span>—(1) The prohibition in <a href="#regulation-14" title="Go to reg. 14">regulation 14</a> (asset-freeze in relation to designated persons) is not contravened by an independent person (“<span class="LegTerm" id="term-p">P</span>”) transferring to another person a legal or equitable interest in funds or economic resources where, immediately before the transfer, the interest—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-22-1-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">is held by P, and</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-22-1-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">is not held jointly with the designated person.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(2) In <a href="#regulation-22-1" title="Go to reg. 22(1)">paragraph (1)</a>, “<span class="LegTerm" id="term-independent-person">independent person</span>” means a person who—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-22-2-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">is not the designated person, and</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-22-2-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">is not owned or controlled directly or indirectly (within the meaning of <a href="#regulation-9" title="Go to reg. 9">regulation 9</a> (meaning of “<span class="LegTerm" id="term-owned-or-controlled-directly-or-indirectly">owned or controlled directly or indirectly</span>”)) by the designated person.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(3) The prohibitions in <a href="#regulation-14" title="Go to reg. 14">regulations 14</a> to <a href="#regulation-16" title="Go to reg. 16">16</a> (asset-freeze in relation to, and making funds available to, or for the benefit of, designated persons) are not contravened by a relevant institution crediting a frozen account with interest or other earnings due on the account.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(4) The prohibitions in <a href="#regulation-15" title="Go to reg. 15">regulations 15</a> and <a href="#regulation-16" title="Go to reg. 16">16</a> (making funds available to, or for the benefit of, designated persons) are not contravened by a relevant institution crediting a frozen account where it receives funds transferred to that institution for crediting to that account.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(5) The prohibitions in <a href="#regulation-15" title="Go to reg. 15">regulations 15</a> and <a href="#regulation-16" title="Go to reg. 16">16</a> are not contravened by the transfer of funds to a relevant institution for crediting to an account held or controlled (directly or indirectly) by a designated person, where those funds are transferred in discharge (or partial discharge) of an obligation which arose before the date on which the person became a designated person.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(6) The prohibitions in <a href="#regulation-14" title="Go to reg. 14">regulations 14</a> to <a href="#regulation-16" title="Go to reg. 16">16</a> are not contravened in relation to a designated person (“<span class="LegTerm" id="term-p">P</span>”) by a transfer of funds from account A to account B, where—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-22-6-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">account A is with a relevant institution which carries on an excluded activity within the meaning of <a class="LegCitation" title="Go to item of legislation" rel="cite" href="https://www.legislation.gov.uk/ukpga/2000/8/section/142D">section 142D</a> (excluded activities) of the <a class="LegCitation" title="Go to item of legislation" rel="cite" href="https://www.legislation.gov.uk/ukpga/2000/8/contents">Financial Services and Markets Act 2000</a>(<a class="LegFootnoteRef" href="#f00006" title="Go to footnote 6" id="Backf00006">6</a>),</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-22-6-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">account B is with a ring-fenced body within the meaning of <a class="LegCitation" title="Go to item of legislation" rel="cite" href="https://www.legislation.gov.uk/ukpga/2000/8/section/142A">section 142A</a> (“ring-fenced body”) of the <a class="LegCitation" title="Go to item of legislation" rel="cite" href="https://www.legislation.gov.uk/ukpga/2000/8/contents">Financial Services and Markets Act 2000</a>(<a class="LegFootnoteRef" href="#f00007" title="Go to footnote 7" id="Backf00007">7</a>), and</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-22-6-c">(c)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">accounts A and B are held or controlled (directly or indirectly) by P.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(7) In <a href="#regulation-22" title="Go to reg. 22">this regulation</a>—&#xD;</p><a class="LegAnchorID" id="p01559"></a><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3">“<span class="LegTerm" id="term-designated-person">designated person</span>” has the same meaning as it has in <a href="#part-3" title="Go to Pt. 3">Part 3</a> (Finance);&#xD;</p><a class="LegAnchorID" id="p01564"></a><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3">“<span class="LegTerm" id="term-frozen-account">frozen account</span>” means an account with a relevant institution which is held or controlled (directly or indirectly) by a designated person;&#xD;</p><a class="LegAnchorID" id="p01568"></a><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3">“<span class="LegTerm" id="term-relevant-institution">relevant institution</span>” means a person that has permission under <a class="LegCitation" title="Go to item of legislation" rel="cite" href="https://www.legislation.gov.uk/ukpga/2000/8/part/4A">Part 4A</a> (Permission to carry on regulated activities) of the <a class="LegCitation" title="Go to item of legislation" rel="cite" href="https://www.legislation.gov.uk/ukpga/2000/8/contents">Financial Services and Markets Act 2000</a>(<a class="LegFootnoteRef" href="#f00008" title="Go to footnote 8" id="Backf00008">8</a>).&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(8) The definition of “relevant institution” in <a href="#regulation-22-7" title="Go to reg. 22(7)">paragraph (7)</a> is to be read with <a class="LegCitation" title="Go to item of legislation" rel="cite" href="https://www.legislation.gov.uk/ukpga/2000/8/section/22">section 22</a> (regulated activities) of the <a class="LegCitation" title="Go to item of legislation" rel="cite" href="https://www.legislation.gov.uk/ukpga/2000/8/contents">Financial Services and Markets Act 2000</a>(<a class="LegFootnoteRef" href="#f00009" title="Go to footnote 9" id="Backf00009">9</a>), any relevant order under <a class="LegCitation" title="Go to item of legislation" rel="cite" href="https://www.legislation.gov.uk/ukpga/2000/8/section/22">that section</a>(<a class="LegFootnoteRef" href="#f00010" title="Go to footnote 10" id="Backf00010">10</a>) and Schedule 2 (regulated activities) to that Act(<a class="LegFootnoteRef" href="#f00011" title="Go to footnote 11" id="Backf00011">11</a>).&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(9) For the purposes of <a href="#regulation-22-1" title="Go to reg. 22(1)">paragraphs (1)</a><a href="#regulation-22-1-b" title="Go to reg. 22(1)(b)">(b)</a>, <a href="#regulation-22-5" title="Go to reg. 22(5)">(5)</a>, and <a href="#regulation-22-6" title="Go to reg. 22(6)">(6)</a> and the definition of “frozen account” in <a href="#regulation-22-7" title="Go to reg. 22(7)">paragraph (7)</a>—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-22-9-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">references to a designated person are to be read as including a person (“<span class="LegTerm" id="term-c">C</span>”) who is owned or controlled directly or indirectly by the designated person (“<span class="LegTerm" id="term-d">D</span>”) in accordance with regulation 9 (meaning of “<span class="LegTerm" id="term-owned-or-controlled-directly-or-indirectly">owned or controlled directly or indirectly</span>”), and</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-22-9-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">in respect of C, the reference in paragraph (5) to the “date on which the person became a designated person” is to be read as the date on which D became a designated person.</span>&#xD;</p><div class="LegAnnotations"><p class="LegAnnotationsGroupHeading">Commencement Information</p><div class="LegCommentaryItem" id="commentary-key-4b59013ef7b7b298fd28b102b811f370"><p class="LegCommentaryPara"><span class="LegCommentaryType">I22</span><span class="LegCommentaryText">Reg. 22 in force at 15.10.2026, see <a class="LegCitation" href="/id/uksi/2026/1071/regulation/1/2" title="Go to reg. 1(2)" rel="cite"><strong>reg. 1(2)</strong></a></span></p></div></div><h3 class="LegP1GroupTitle">Finance: exception from prohibitions for required payments<span class="LegExtentRestriction" title="Applies to "><span class="btr"></span>U.K.<span class="bbl"></span><span class="bbr"></span></span></h3><p class="LegP1ParaText"><span class="LegP1No" id="regulation-23">23.</span>—(1) This regulation applies to required payments within the meaning of paragraph (2).&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(2) A required payment is a payment which—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-23-2-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">a designated person is required to make under or by virtue of any enactment to—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4No" id="regulation-23-2-a-i">(i)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4Text">the registrar of companies,</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4No" id="regulation-23-2-a-ii">(ii)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4Text">the Commissioners for His Majesty's Revenue and Customs,</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4No" id="regulation-23-2-a-iii">(iii)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4Text">the Welsh Revenue Authority,</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4No" id="regulation-23-2-a-iv">(iv)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4Text">Revenue Scotland,</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4No" id="regulation-23-2-a-v">(v)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4Text">the Financial Conduct Authority,</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4No" id="regulation-23-2-a-vi">(vi)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4Text">the Secretary of State,</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4No" id="regulation-23-2-a-vii">(vii)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4Text">the Welsh Ministers,</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4No" id="regulation-23-2-a-viii">(viii)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4Text">the Department of Finance in Northern Ireland, or</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4No" id="regulation-23-2-a-ix">(ix)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4Text">a local authority, and</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-23-2-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">is not an excluded payment.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(3) The prohibitions in regulations 14 (asset-freeze in relation to designated persons) and 16 (making funds available for benefit of designated person) are not contravened by a person making a required payment.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(4) Where a required payment is made by a person other than a designated person, the prohibition in regulation 14 is not contravened by the designated person making a reimbursement payment to that person.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(5) The reference in paragraph (3) to a person making a required payment includes a designated person, but only where they are making a required payment on their own behalf.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(6) The following payments are to be treated as payments which a designated person is required to make under or by virtue of an enactment for the purposes of this regulation, where made by a designated person on their own behalf or by a person, other than a designated person, on behalf of a designated person—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-23-6-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">a payment to the Financial Conduct Authority of a levy imposed by the scheme manager of the Financial Services Compensation Scheme by virtue of section 213 (the compensation scheme) of the Financial Services and Markets Act 2000(<a class="LegFootnoteRef" href="#f00012" title="Go to footnote 12" id="Backf00012">12</a>);</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-23-6-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">a payment to the Financial Conduct Authority which is collected by that Authority on behalf of the Financial Reporting Council Limited(<a class="LegFootnoteRef" href="#f00013" title="Go to footnote 13" id="Backf00013">13</a>).</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(7) For the purposes of this regulation, references to a designated person are to be read as including a person who is owned or controlled directly or indirectly (within the meaning of regulation 9 (meaning of “<span class="LegTerm" id="term-owned-or-controlled-directly-or-indirectly">owned or controlled directly or indirectly</span>”)) by the designated person.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(8) In this regulation—&#xD;</p><a class="LegAnchorID" id="p01751"></a><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3">“<span class="LegTerm" id="term-bid-levy">BID levy</span>” means a levy that is imposed on those persons who are, in respect of particular business improvement district proposals, entitled to vote in accordance with section 39(3) (entitlement to vote in ballot) of the Planning etc. (Scotland) Act 2006(<a class="LegFootnoteRef" href="#f00014" title="Go to footnote 14" id="Backf00014">14</a>);&#xD;</p><a class="LegAnchorID" id="p01757"></a><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3">“<span class="LegTerm" id="term-business-improvement-district">business improvement district</span>” has the meaning given in section 33 (arrangements with respect to business improvement districts) of the Planning etc. (Scotland) Act 2006;&#xD;</p><a class="LegAnchorID" id="p01761"></a><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3">“<span class="LegTerm" id="term-designated-person">designated person</span>” has the same meaning as it has in Part 3 (Finance);&#xD;</p><a class="LegAnchorID" id="p01765"></a><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3">“<span class="LegTerm" id="term-enactment">enactment</span>” has the meaning given in section 54(6) (regulations: general) of the Act(<a class="LegFootnoteRef" href="#f00015" title="Go to footnote 15" id="Backf00015">15</a>);&#xD;</p><a class="LegAnchorID" id="p01771"></a><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3">“<span class="LegCommentaryPara"><span class="LegCommentaryType">I23</span><span class="LegCommentaryText">Reg. 23 in force at 15.10.2026, see <a class="LegCitation" href="/id/uksi/2026/1071/regulation/1/2" title="Go to reg. 1(2)" rel="cite"><strong>reg. 1(2)</strong></a></span></p></div></div><h3 class="LegP1GroupTitle">Finance: exception for authorised conduct in a relevant country<span class="LegExtentRestriction" title="Applies to "><span class="btr"></span>U.K.<span class="bbl"></span><span class="bbr"></span></span></h3><p class="LegP1ParaText"><span class="LegP1No" id="regulation-24">24.</span>—(1) Where a person's conduct in a relevant country would, in the absence of <a href="#regulation-27" title="Go to reg. 27">this regulation</a>, contravene a prohibition in any of  <a href="#regulation-14" title="Go to reg. 14">regulations 14</a> to <a href="#regulation-18" title="Go to reg. 18">18</a> (asset-freeze etc.) (“the relevant prohibition”), the relevant prohibition is not contravened if the conduct is authorised by a licence or other authorisation which is—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-24-1-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">issued under the law of the relevant country, and</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-24-1-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">for the purpose of disapplying a prohibition in that jurisdiction which corresponds to the relevant prohibition.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(2) In <a href="#regulation-27" title="Go to reg. 27">this regulation</a>, “<span class="LegTerm" id="term-relevant-country">relevant country</span>” means—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-24-2-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">any of the Channel Islands;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-24-2-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">the Isle of Man;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-24-2-c">(c)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">any British overseas territory.</span>&#xD;</p><div class="LegAnnotations"><p class="LegAnnotationsGroupHeading">Commencement Information</p><div class="LegCommentaryItem" id="commentary-key-ed2ef04f1808e873e4907258ed42c3f3"><p class="LegCommentaryPara"><span class="LegCommentaryType">I24</span><span class="LegCommentaryText">Reg. 24 in force at 15.10.2026, see <a class="LegCitation" href="/id/uksi/2026/1071/regulation/1/2" title="Go to reg. 1(2)" rel="cite"><strong>reg. 1(2)</strong></a></span></p></div></div><h3 class="LegP1GroupTitle">Exception for acts done for purposes of national security or prevention of serious crime<span class="LegExtentRestriction" title="Applies to "><span class="btr"></span>U.K.<span class="bbl"></span><span class="bbr"></span></span></h3><p class="LegP1ParaText"><span class="LegP1No" id="regulation-25">25.</span>—(1) Where an act would, in the absence of <a href="#regulation-23-1" title="Go to reg. 23(1)">this paragraph</a>, be prohibited by <a href="#regulation-12" title="Go to reg. 12">regulation 12</a><a href="#regulation-12-2" title="Go to reg. 12(2)">(2)</a> (confidentiality) or any prohibition in <a href="#part-3" title="Go to Pt. 3">Part 3</a> (Finance), that prohibition does not apply to the act if the act is one which a responsible officer has determined would be in the interests of—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-25-1-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">national security, or</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-25-1-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">the prevention or detection of serious crime in the United Kingdom or elsewhere.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(2) Where, in the absence of <a href="#regulation-23-2" title="Go to reg. 23(2)">this paragraph</a>, a thing would be required to be done under or by virtue of a provision of <a href="#part-7" title="Go to Pt. 7">Part 7</a> (Information and records), that requirement does not apply if a responsible officer has determined that not doing the thing in question would be in the interests of—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-25-2-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">national security, or</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-25-2-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">the prevention or detection of serious crime in the United Kingdom or elsewhere.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(3) In <a href="#regulation-23" title="Go to reg. 23">this regulation</a>, “<span class="LegTerm" id="term-responsible-officer">responsible officer</span>” means a person in the service of the Crown or holding office under the Crown, acting in the course of that person's duty.&#xD;</p><div class="LegAnnotations"><p class="LegAnnotationsGroupHeading">Commencement Information</p><div class="LegCommentaryItem" id="commentary-key-570ceb7ec91ea9df91b9fd5c25804abb"><p class="LegCommentaryPara"><span class="LegCommentaryType">I25</span><span class="LegCommentaryText">Reg. 25 in force at 15.10.2026, see <a class="LegCitation" href="/id/uksi/2026/1071/regulation/1/2" title="Go to reg. 1(2)" rel="cite"><strong>reg. 1(2)</strong></a></span></p></div></div><h3 class="LegP1GroupTitle">Treasury licences<span class="LegExtentRestriction" title="Applies to "><span class="btr"></span>U.K.<span class="bbl"></span><span class="bbr"></span></span></h3><p class="LegP1ParaText"><span class="LegP1No" id="regulation-26">26.</span>—(1) The prohibitions in  <a href="#regulation-14" title="Go to reg. 14">regulations 14</a> to <a href="#regulation-18" title="Go to reg. 18">18</a> (asset-freeze etc.) do not apply to anything done under the authority of a licence issued by the Treasury under <a href="#regulation-26-1" title="Go to reg. 26(1)">this paragraph</a>.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(2) The Treasury may issue a licence which authorises acts by a particular person only where the Treasury consider that it is appropriate to issue the licence for a purpose set out in <a href="#schedule-2" title="Go to Sch. 2">Schedule 2</a> (Treasury licences: purposes).&#xD;</p><div class="LegAnnotations"><p class="LegAnnotationsGroupHeading">Commencement Information</p><div class="LegCommentaryItem" id="commentary-key-9f9ad6391e8a9b184e84c5e0b44fb984"><p class="LegCommentaryPara"><span class="LegCommentaryType">I26</span><span class="LegCommentaryText">Reg. 26 in force at 15.10.2026, see <a class="LegCitation" href="/id/uksi/2026/1071/regulation/1/2" title="Go to reg. 1(2)" rel="cite"><strong>reg. 1(2)</strong></a></span></p></div></div><h3 class="LegP1GroupTitle">Director disqualification licences<span class="LegExtentRestriction" title="Applies to "><span class="btr"></span>U.K.<span class="bbl"></span><span class="bbr"></span></span></h3><p class="LegP1ParaText"><span class="LegP1No" id="regulation-27">27.</span>  The Secretary of State may issue a licence in relation to any person who is designated under <a href="#regulation-5" title="Go to reg. 5">regulation 5</a> (power to designate persons) for the purposes of <a href="#regulation-20" title="Go to reg. 20">regulation 20</a> (director disqualification sanctions) providing that the prohibitions in—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-27-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">section 11A (designated persons under sanctions legislation) of the Company Directors Disqualification Act 1986, and</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-27-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">Article 15A (designated persons under sanctions legislation) of the Company Directors Disqualification (Northern Ireland) Order 2002,</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegText">do not apply to anything done under the authority of that licence.&#xD;</p><div class="LegAnnotations"><p class="LegAnnotationsGroupHeading">Commencement Information</p><div class="LegCommentaryItem" id="commentary-key-68a51210105989e896a4480affd34ec0"><p class="LegCommentaryPara"><span class="LegCommentaryType">I27</span><span class="LegCommentaryText">Reg. 27 in force at 15.10.2026, see <a class="LegCitation" href="/id/uksi/2026/1071/regulation/1/2" title="Go to reg. 1(2)" rel="cite"><strong>reg. 1(2)</strong></a></span></p></div></div><h3 class="LegP1GroupTitle">Licences: general provisions<span class="LegExtentRestriction" title="Applies to "><span class="btr"></span>U.K.<span class="bbl"></span><span class="bbr"></span></span></h3><p class="LegP1ParaText"><span class="LegP1No" id="regulation-28">28.</span>—(1) <a href="#regulation-26" title="Go to reg. 26">This regulation</a> applies in relation to Treasury licences and director disqualification licences.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(2) A licence must specify the acts authorised by it.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(3) A licence may be general or may authorise acts by a particular person or persons of a particular description.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(4) A licence may—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-28-4-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">contain conditions;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-28-4-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">be of indefinite duration or a defined duration.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(5) A person who issues a licence may vary, revoke or suspend it at any time.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(6) A person who issues, varies, revokes or suspends a licence which authorises acts by a particular person must give written notice to that person of the issue, variation, revocation or suspension of the licence.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(7) A person who issues, varies, revokes or suspends a general licence or a licence which authorises acts by persons of a particular description must take such steps as that person considers appropriate to publicise the issue, variation, revocation or suspension of the licence.&#xD;</p><div class="LegAnnotations"><p class="LegAnnotationsGroupHeading">Commencement Information</p><div class="LegCommentaryItem" id="commentary-key-a9d07f1aaf6af121c3d68d37299d1162"><p class="LegCommentaryPara"><span class="LegCommentaryType">I28</span><span class="LegCommentaryText">Reg. 28 in force at 15.10.2026, see <a class="LegCitation" href="/id/uksi/2026/1071/regulation/1/2" title="Go to reg. 1(2)" rel="cite"><strong>reg. 1(2)</strong></a></span></p></div></div><h3 class="LegP1GroupTitle">Notices<span class="LegExtentRestriction" title="Applies to "><span class="btr"></span>U.K.<span class="bbl"></span><span class="bbr"></span></span></h3><p class="LegP1ParaText"><span class="LegP1No" id="regulation-29">29.</span>—(1) <a href="#regulation-28" title="Go to reg. 28">This regulation</a> applies in relation to a notice required by <a href="#regulation-28" title="Go to reg. 28">regulation 28</a><a href="#regulation-28-6" title="Go to reg. 28(6)">(6)</a> (licences: general provisions) to be given to a person.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(2) The notice may be given to an individual—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-29-2-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">by delivering it to the individual,</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-29-2-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">by sending it to the individual by post addressed to the individual at his or her usual or last-known place of residence or business, or</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-29-2-c">(c)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">by leaving it for the individual at that place.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(3) The notice may be given to a person other than an individual—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-29-3-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">by sending it by post to the proper officer of the body at its principal office, or</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-29-3-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">by addressing it to the proper officer of the body and leaving it at that office.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(4) The notice may be given to the person by electronic means.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(5) The notice may be given to the person by other means with the person's consent.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(6) In <a href="#regulation-28" title="Go to reg. 28">this regulation</a>, the reference in <a href="#regulation-28-3" title="Go to reg. 28(3)">paragraph (3)</a> to a “principal office”—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-29-6-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">in relation to a registered company, is to be read as a reference to the company's registered office;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-29-6-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">in relation to a body incorporated or constituted under the law of a country other than the United Kingdom, includes a reference to the body's principal office in the United Kingdom (if any).</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(7) In <a href="#regulation-28" title="Go to reg. 28">this regulation</a>—&#xD;</p><a class="LegAnchorID" id="p02175"></a><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3">“<span class="LegTerm" id="term-proper-officer">proper officer</span>”—&#xD;</p><div><div class="LegAlphaList"><div class="LegListItem" id="p02178"><div class="LegLevel4No LegListItemNo">(a)</div><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel4">in relation to a body other than a partnership, means the secretary or other executive officer charged with the conduct of the body’s general affairs, and&#xD;</p></div><div class="LegListItem" id="p02182"><div class="LegLevel4No LegListItemNo">(b)</div><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel4">in relation to a partnership, means a partner or a person who has the control or management of the partnership business;&#xD;</p></div></div></div><a class="LegAnchorID" id="p02187"></a><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3">“<span class="LegTerm" id="term-registered-company">registered company</span>” means a company registered under the enactments relating to companies for the time being in force in the United Kingdom.&#xD;</p><div class="LegAnnotations"><p class="LegAnnotationsGroupHeading">Commencement Information</p><div class="LegCommentaryItem" id="commentary-key-d2d11c4ee5eeef6748c9b85fbde9bc54"><p class="LegCommentaryPara"><span class="LegCommentaryType">I29</span><span class="LegCommentaryText">Reg. 29 in force at 15.10.2026, see <a class="LegCitation" href="/id/uksi/2026/1071/regulation/1/2" title="Go to reg. 1(2)" rel="cite"><strong>reg. 1(2)</strong></a></span></p></div></div><h3 class="LegP1GroupTitle">Finance: licensing offences<span class="LegExtentRestriction" title="Applies to "><span class="btr"></span>U.K.<span class="bbl"></span><span class="bbr"></span></span></h3><p class="LegP1ParaText"><span class="LegP1No" id="regulation-30">30.</span>—(1) A person (“<span class="LegTerm" id="term-p">P</span>”) commits an offence if P knowingly or recklessly—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-30-1-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">provides information that is false in a material respect, or</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-30-1-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">provides or produces a document that is not what it purports to be,</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2Text">for the purpose of obtaining a Treasury licence (whether for P or anyone else).&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(2) A person who purports to act under the authority of a Treasury licence but who fails to comply with any condition of the licence commits an offence.&#xD;</p><div class="LegAnnotations"><p class="LegAnnotationsGroupHeading">Commencement Information</p><div class="LegCommentaryItem" id="commentary-key-83e917eaff4ae7e9b8d333addf4f4018"><p class="LegCommentaryPara"><span class="LegCommentaryType">I30</span><span class="LegCommentaryText">Reg. 30 in force at 15.10.2026, see <a class="LegCitation" href="/id/uksi/2026/1071/regulation/1/2" title="Go to reg. 1(2)" rel="cite"><strong>reg. 1(2)</strong></a></span></p></div></div><h3 class="LegP1GroupTitle">Director disqualification: licensing offences<span class="LegExtentRestriction" title="Applies to "><span class="btr"></span>U.K.<span class="bbl"></span><span class="bbr"></span></span></h3><p class="LegP1ParaText"><span class="LegP1No" id="regulation-31">31.</span>—(1) A person (“<span class="LegTerm" id="term-p">P</span>”) commits an offence if P knowingly or recklessly—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-31-1-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">provides information that is false in a material respect, or</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-31-1-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">provides or produces a document that is not what it purports to be,</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2Text">for the purpose of obtaining a director disqualification licence (whether for P or anyone else).&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(2) A person who purports to act under the authority of a director disqualification licence but who fails to comply with any condition of the licence commits an offence.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(3) A licence in respect of which an offence under <a href="#regulation-30-1" title="Go to reg. 30(1)">paragraph (1)</a> has been committed is to be treated as void from the time at which it was issued.&#xD;</p><div class="LegAnnotations"><p class="LegAnnotationsGroupHeading">Commencement Information</p><div class="LegCommentaryItem" id="commentary-key-22fe8a3772f3d325610509d5815e19e7"><p class="LegCommentaryPara"><span class="LegCommentaryType">I31</span><span class="LegCommentaryText">Reg. 31 in force at 15.10.2026, see <a class="LegCitation" href="/id/uksi/2026/1071/regulation/1/2" title="Go to reg. 1(2)" rel="cite"><strong>reg. 1(2)</strong></a></span></p></div></div><h3 class="LegP1GroupTitle">Section 8B(1) to (3) of the Immigration Act 1971: directions<span class="LegExtentRestriction" title="Applies to "><span class="btr"></span>U.K.<span class="bbl"></span><span class="bbr"></span></span></h3><p class="LegP1ParaText"><span class="LegP1No" id="regulation-32">32.</span>—(1) The Secretary of State may direct that, in relation to any person within <a href="#regulation-21" title="Go to reg. 21">regulation 21</a> (immigration) whose name is specified, or who is of a specified description, section 8B(1) and (2) (persons excluded from the United Kingdom under certain instruments) of the Immigration Act 1971, or section 8B(3) of that Act, have effect subject to specified exceptions.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(2) A direction under <a href="#regulation-31" title="Go to reg. 31">this regulation</a>—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-32-2-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">may contain conditions;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-32-2-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">must be of a defined duration (and that duration may be expressed in any way, including, for example, being expressed in a way such that the direction ceases to have effect on, or within a specified period after, the occurrence of a specified event).</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(3) The Secretary of State may vary, revoke or suspend a direction under <a href="#regulation-31" title="Go to reg. 31">this regulation</a> at any time.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(4) On the issue, variation, revocation or suspension of a direction under <a href="#regulation-31" title="Go to reg. 31">this regulation</a>, the Secretary of State may take such steps as the Secretary of State considers appropriate to publicise the issue, variation, revocation or suspension of the direction.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(5) In <a href="#regulation-31" title="Go to reg. 31">this regulation</a>, “<span class="LegTerm" id="term-specified">specified</span>” means specified in a direction under <a href="#regulation-31" title="Go to reg. 31">this regulation</a>.&#xD;</p><div class="LegAnnotations"><p
