class="LegCommentaryType">I32</span><span class="LegCommentaryText">Reg. 32 in force at 15.10.2026, see <a class="LegCitation" href="/id/uksi/2026/1071/regulation/1/2" title="Go to reg. 1(2)" rel="cite"><strong>reg. 1(2)</strong></a></span></p></div></div><a class="LegAnchorID" id="part-7"></a><div class="LegClearPart"></div><h2 class="LegPart"><span class="LegPartNo">Part 7<span class="LegExtentRestriction" title="Applies to "><span class="btr"></span>U.K.<span class="bbl"></span><span class="bbr"></span></span></span><span class="LegPartTitle">Information and records</span></h2><h3 class="LegP1GroupTitleFirst">Finance: reporting obligations<span class="LegExtentRestriction" title="Applies to "><span class="btr"></span>U.K.<span class="bbl"></span><span class="bbr"></span></span></h3><p class="LegP1ParaText"><span class="LegP1No" id="regulation-33">33.</span>—(1) A relevant firm must inform the Treasury as soon as practicable if—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-33-1-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">it knows, or has reasonable cause to suspect, that a person—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4No" id="regulation-33-1-a-i">(i)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4Text">is a designated person, or</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4No" id="regulation-33-1-a-ii">(ii)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4Text">has breached a prohibition or failed to comply with an obligation under any provision of <a href="#part-3" title="Go to Pt. 3">Part 3</a> (Finance) or <a href="#regulation-30" title="Go to reg. 30">regulation 30</a> (finance: licensing offences), and</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-33-1-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">the information or other matter on which the knowledge or cause for suspicion is based came to it in the course of carrying on its business.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(2) Where a relevant firm informs the Treasury under <a href="#regulation-33-1" title="Go to reg. 33(1)">paragraph (1)</a>, it must state—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-33-2-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">the information or other matter on which the knowledge or suspicion is based, and</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-33-2-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">any information it holds about the person by which the person can be identified.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(3) <a href="#regulation-33-4" title="Go to reg. 33(4)">Paragraph (4)</a> applies if—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-33-3-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">a relevant firm informs the Treasury under <a href="#regulation-33-1" title="Go to reg. 33(1)">paragraph (1)</a> that it knows, or has reasonable cause to suspect, that a person is a designated person, and</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-33-3-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">that person is a customer of the relevant firm.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(4) The relevant firm must also state the nature and amount or quantity of any funds or economic resources held by it for the customer at the time when it first had the knowledge or suspicion.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(5) Where a person (“<span class="LegTerm" id="term-p">P</span>”) knows, or has reasonable cause to suspect, that P holds funds or economic resources owned, held or controlled by a designated person, P must by no later than 30th November in each calendar year provide a report to the Treasury as to the nature and amount or quantity of those funds or economic resources held by P as of 30th September in that calendar year.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(6) Where a report has been provided further to <a href="#regulation-33-5" title="Go to reg. 33(5)">paragraph (5)</a> but as of 30th September in the following calendar year P no longer holds funds or economic resources owned, held or controlled by the designated person, P must no later than 30th November in that calendar year report this to the Treasury.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(7) For the purposes of <a href="#regulation-33-5" title="Go to reg. 33(5)">paragraphs (5)</a> and <a href="#regulation-33-6" title="Go to reg. 33(6)">(6)</a>, funds or economic resources are to be treated as owned, held or controlled by a designated person if they are owned, held or controlled by a person who is owned or controlled directly or indirectly (within the meaning of <a href="#regulation-9" title="Go to reg. 9">regulation 9</a> (meaning of “<span class="LegTerm" id="term-owned-or-controlled-directly-or-indirectly">owned or controlled directly or indirectly</span>”)) by the designated person.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(8) A relevant institution must inform the Treasury without delay if that institution—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-33-8-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">credits a frozen account in accordance with <a href="#regulation-22" title="Go to reg. 22">regulation 22</a><a href="#regulation-22-4" title="Go to reg. 22(4)">(4)</a> (finance: exceptions from prohibitions), or</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-33-8-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">transfers funds from a frozen account in accordance with <a href="#regulation-22" title="Go to reg. 22">regulation 22</a><a href="#regulation-22-6" title="Go to reg. 22(6)">(6)</a>.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(9) A person who fails to comply with a requirement in <a href="#regulation-33-1" title="Go to reg. 33(1)">paragraph (1)</a>, <a href="#regulation-33-2" title="Go to reg. 33(2)">(2)</a> or <a href="#regulation-33-4" title="Go to reg. 33(4)">(4)</a> commits an offence.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(10) A person commits an offence if that person, without reasonable excuse, fails to comply with a requirement in <a href="#regulation-33-5" title="Go to reg. 33(5)">paragraph (5)</a> or <a href="#regulation-33-6" title="Go to reg. 33(6)">(6)</a>.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(11) In <a href="#regulation-33" title="Go to reg. 33">this regulation</a>—&#xD;</p><a class="LegAnchorID" id="p02389"></a><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3">“<span class="LegTerm" id="term-designated-person">designated person</span>” has the same meaning as it has in <a href="#part-3" title="Go to Pt. 3">Part 3</a>;&#xD;</p><a class="LegAnchorID" id="p02394"></a><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3">“<span class="LegTerm" id="term-frozen-account">frozen account</span>” has the same meaning as it has in <a href="#regulation-22" title="Go to reg. 22">regulation 22</a>;&#xD;</p><a class="LegAnchorID" id="p02399"></a><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3">“<span class="LegTerm" id="term-relevant-firm">relevant firm</span>” is to be read in accordance with <a href="#regulation-35" title="Go to reg. 35">regulation 35</a> (“relevant firm”);&#xD;</p><a class="LegAnchorID" id="p02404"></a><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3">“<span class="LegTerm" id="term-relevant-institution">relevant institution</span>” has the same meaning as it has in <a href="#regulation-22" title="Go to reg. 22">regulation 22</a>.&#xD;</p><div class="LegAnnotations"><p class="LegAnnotationsGroupHeading">Commencement Information</p><div class="LegCommentaryItem" id="commentary-key-b347b7f33f2e1d993d31baef908495c1"><p class="LegCommentaryPara"><span class="LegCommentaryType">I33</span><span class="LegCommentaryText">Reg. 33 in force at 15.10.2026, see <a class="LegCitation" href="/id/uksi/2026/1071/regulation/1/2" title="Go to reg. 1(2)" rel="cite"><strong>reg. 1(2)</strong></a></span></p></div></div><h3 class="LegP1GroupTitle">Finance: reporting obligations for required payments<span class="LegExtentRestriction" title="Applies to "><span class="btr"></span>U.K.<span class="bbl"></span><span class="bbr"></span></span></h3><p class="LegP1ParaText"><span class="LegP1No" id="regulation-34">34.</span>—(1) A designated person must inform the Treasury without delay if they make a required payment.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(2) A person who makes a required payment on behalf of a designated person must inform the Treasury without delay that they have made the required payment.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(3) A person must inform the Treasury without delay if they receive a reimbursement payment.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(4) For the purposes of this regulation, references to a designated person are to be read as including a person who is owned or controlled directly or indirectly (within the meaning of regulation 9 (meaning of “<span class="LegTerm" id="term-owned-or-controlled-directly-or-indirectly">owned or controlled directly or indirectly</span>”)) by a designated person.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(5) In this regulation—&#xD;</p><a class="LegAnchorID" id="p02432"></a><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3">“<span regulation 23(2).&#xD;</p><div class="LegAnnotations"><p class="LegAnnotationsGroupHeading">Commencement Information</p><div class="LegCommentaryItem" class="LegCitation" href="/id/uksi/2026/1071/regulation/1/2" title="Go to reg. 1(2)" rel="cite"><strong>reg. 1(2)</strong></a></span></p></div></div><h3 class="LegP1GroupTitle">“Relevant firm”<span payments)—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-35-1-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">a person that has permission under <a value) by any means, or</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4No" id="regulation-35-1-b-iii">(iii)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4Text">cashes cheques that are made payable to customers;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-35-1-c">(c)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS (Statutory Auditors) of the <a class="LegCitation" title="Go to item of legislation" rel="cite" href="https://www.legislation.gov.uk/ukpga/2006/46/contents">Companies Act 2006</a>(<a LegP4Text">advice about tax affairs, or</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4No" id="regulation-35-1-d-iv">(iv)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4Text">trust or company services within the meaning of <a href="#regulation-35-2" title="Go to reg. 35(2)">paragraph (2)</a>;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-35-1-e">(e)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">a firm or sole practitioner that carries out, or whose employees carry out, estate agency work;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-35-1-f">(f)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">the holder of a casino operating licence within the meaning given by <a class="LegCitation" title="Go to item of legislation" rel="cite" href="https://www.legislation.gov.uk/ukpga/2005/19/section/65">section 65</a> <a class="LegCitation" title="Go to item of legislation" rel="cite" href="https://www.legislation.gov.uk/ukpga/2005/19/section/65">(2)</a> <a class="LegCitation" title="Go to item of legislation" rel="cite" href="https://www.legislation.gov.uk/ukpga/2005/19/section/65">(a)</a> (nature of a licence) of the <a class="LegCitation" title="Go to item of legislation" rel="cite" href="https://www.legislation.gov.uk/ukpga/2005/19/contents">Gambling Act 2005</a>(<a class="LegFootnoteRef" href="#f00034" title="Go to footnote 34" id="Backf00034">34</a>);</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-35-1-g">(g)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">a person engaged in the business of making, supplying, selling (including selling by auction) or exchanging—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4No" id="regulation-35-1-g-i">(i)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4Text">articles made from gold, silver, platinum or palladium, or</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4No" id="regulation-35-1-g-ii">(ii)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4Text">precious stones or pearls;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-35-1-h">(h)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">a cryptoasset exchange provider;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-35-1-i">(i)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">a custodian wallet provider;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-35-1-j">(j)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">a high value dealer;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-35-1-k">(k)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">an art market participant;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-35-1-l">(l)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">an insolvency practitioner;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-35-1-m">(m)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">a firm or sole practitioner (“<span class="LegTerm" id="term-p">P</span>”) that carries out, or whose employees carry out, letting agency work.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(2) In <a href="#regulation-35-1" title="Go to reg. 35(1)">paragraph (1)</a> “<span class="LegTerm" id="term-trust-or-company-services">trust or company services</span>” means any of the following services—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-35-2-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">forming companies or other legal persons;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-35-2-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">acting, or arranging for another person to act—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4No" id="regulation-35-2-b-i">(i)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4Text">as a director or secretary of a company,</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4No" id="regulation-35-2-b-ii">(ii)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4Text">as a partner of a partnership, or</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4No" id="regulation-35-2-b-iii">(iii)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4Text">in a similar capacity in relation to other legal persons;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-35-2-c">(c)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">providing a registered office, business address, correspondence or administrative address or other related services for a company, partnership or any other legal person or arrangement;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-35-2-d">(d)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">acting, or arranging for another person to act, as—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4No" id="regulation-35-2-d-i">(i)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4Text">a trustee of an express trust or similar legal arrangement, or</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4No" id="regulation-35-2-d-ii">(ii)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4Text">a nominee shareholder for a person.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(3) In <a href="#regulation-35-1" title="Go to reg. 35(1)">paragraph (1)</a>—&#xD;</p><a class="LegAnchorID" id="p02619"></a><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3">“<span class="LegTerm" id="term-estate-agency-work">estate agency work</span>” is to be read in accordance with <a class="LegCitation" title="Go to item of legislation" rel="cite" href="https://www.legislation.gov.uk/ukpga/1979/38/section/1">section 1</a> (estate agency work) of the <a class="LegCitation" title="Go to item of legislation" rel="cite" href="https://www.legislation.gov.uk/ukpga/1979/38/contents">Estate Agents Act 1979</a>(<a class="LegFootnoteRef" href="#f00035" title="Go to footnote 35" id="Backf00035">35</a>), but as if references in <a class="LegCitation" title="Go to item of legislation" rel="cite" href="https://www.legislation.gov.uk/ukpga/1979/38/section/1">that section</a> to disposing of or acquiring an interest in land included (despite anything in <a class="LegCitation" title="Go to item of legislation" rel="cite" href="https://www.legislation.gov.uk/ukpga/1979/38/section/2">section 2</a> (interests in land) of <a class="LegCitation" title="Go to item of legislation" rel="cite" href="https://www.legislation.gov.uk/ukpga/1979/38/contents">that Act</a>) references to disposing of or acquiring an estate or interest in land outside the United Kingdom where that estate or interest is capable of being owned or held as a separate interest;&#xD;</p><a class="LegAnchorID" id="p02635"></a><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3">“<span class="LegTerm" id="term-firm">firm</span>” means any entity that, whether or not a legal person, is not an individual, and includes a body corporate and a partnership or other unincorporated body.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(4) In <a href="#regulation-35-1" title="Go to reg. 35(1)">paragraph (1)</a>, a “<span class="LegTerm" id="term-cryptoasset-exchange-provider">cryptoasset exchange provider</span>” means a firm or sole practitioner that by way of business provides one or more of the following services, including where the firm or sole practitioner does so as creator or issuer of any of the cryptoassets involved—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-35-4-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">exchanging, or arranging or making arrangements with a view to the exchange of, cryptoassets for money or money for cryptoassets,</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-35-4-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">exchanging, or arranging or making arrangements with a view to the exchange of, one cryptoasset for another, or</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-35-4-c">(c)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">operating a machine which utilises automated processes to exchange cryptoassets for money or money for cryptoassets.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(5) In <a href="#regulation-35-1" title="Go to reg. 35(1)">paragraph (1)</a>, a “<span class="LegTerm" id="term-custodian-wallet-provider">custodian wallet provider</span>” means a firm or sole practitioner that by way of business provides services to safeguard, or to safeguard and administer—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-35-5-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">cryptoassets on behalf of its customers, or</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-35-5-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">private cryptographic keys on behalf of its customers in order to hold, store and transfer cryptoassets.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(6) For the purposes of <a href="#regulation-35" title="Go to reg. 35">this regulation</a>—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-35-6-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">“<span class="LegTerm" id="term-cryptoasset">cryptoasset</span>” means a cryptographically secured digital representation of value or contractual rights that uses a form of distributed ledger technology and can be transferred, stored or traded electronically;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-35-6-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">“<span class="LegTerm" id="term-money">money</span>” means—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4No" id="regulation-35-6-b-i">(i)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4Text">money in sterling,</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4No" id="regulation-35-6-b-ii">(ii)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4Text">money in any other currency, or</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4No" id="regulation-35-6-b-iii">(iii)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4Text">money in any other medium of exchange,</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegRHS LegP3Text">but does not include a cryptoasset; and&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-35-6-c">(c)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">in <a href="#regulation-35-4-a" title="Go to reg. 35(4)(a)">sub-paragraphs (a)</a> to <a href="#regulation-35-4-c" title="Go to reg. 35(4)(c)">(c)</a> of <a href="#regulation-35-4" title="Go to reg. 35(4)">paragraph (4)</a>, “<span class="LegTerm" id="term-cryptoasset">cryptoasset</span>” includes a right to, or interest in, the cryptoasset.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(7) In <a href="#regulation-35-1" title="Go to reg. 35(1)">paragraph (1)</a>, a “<span class="LegTerm" id="term-high-value-dealer">high value dealer</span>” means a firm or sole trader that by way of business trades in goods (including an auctioneer dealing in goods), when the firm or sole trader makes or receives, in respect of any transaction, a payment or payments in case of at least £10,000 in total, whether the transaction is excuted in a single operation or in several operations which appear to be linked.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(8) In <a href="#regulation-35-1" title="Go to reg. 35(1)">paragraph (1)</a>, an “<span class="LegTerm" id="term-art-market-participant">art market participant</span>” means, subject to <a href="#regulation-35-9" title="Go to reg. 35(9)">paragraph (9)</a>, a firm or sole practitioner that is registered or required to register with the Commissioners for His Majesty’s Revenue and Customs as an art market participant under <a class="LegCitation" title="Go to item of legislation" rel="cite" href="https://www.legislation.gov.uk/uksi/2017/692/regulation/56">regulations 56</a> <a class="LegCitation" title="Go to item of legislation" rel="cite" href="https://www.legislation.gov.uk/uksi/2017/692/regulation/56">(5)</a> and <a class="LegCitation" title="Go to item of legislation" rel="cite" href="https://www.legislation.gov.uk/uksi/2017/692/regulation/56">(6)</a> (requirement to be registered) of the <a class="LegCitation" title="Go to item of legislation" rel="cite" href="https://www.legislation.gov.uk/uksi/2017/692/contents">Money Laundering, Terrorist Financing and Transfer of Funds (Information on the Payer) Regulations 2017</a>(<a class="LegFootnoteRef" href="#f00036" title="Go to footnote 36" id="Backf00036">36</a>).&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(9) A firm or sole practitioner is not an art market participant for the purposes of <a href="#regulation-35-8" title="Go to reg. 35(8)">paragraph (8)</a> in relation to the sale or storage of a work of art which is created by, or is attributable to, a member of the firm or the sole practitioner.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(10) For the purposes of <a href="#regulation-35" title="Go to reg. 35">this regulation</a>, “<span class="LegTerm" id="term-work-of-art">work of art</span>” means anything which, in accordance with section 21(6) to (6B) (value of imported goods) of the Value Added Tax Act 1994(<a class="LegFootnoteRef" href="#f00037" title="Go to footnote 37" id="Backf00037">37</a>), is a work of art for the purposes of <a class="LegCitation" title="Go to item of legislation" rel="cite" href="">section 21</a> <a class="LegCitation" title="Go to item of legislation" rel="cite" href="">(5)</a> <a class="LegCitation" title="Go to item of legislation" rel="cite" href="">(a)</a> of <a class="LegCitation" title="Go to item of legislation" rel="cite" href="">that Act</a>.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(11) In paragraph (1), an “<span class="LegTerm" id="term-insolvency-practitioner">insolvency practitioner</span>” means a firm or individual who acts as an insolvency practitioner within the meaning of section 388 (meaning of “<span class="LegTerm" id="term-act-as-insolvency-practitioner">act as insolvency practitioner</span>”) of the Insolvency Act 1986(<a class="LegFootnoteRef" href="#f00038" title="Go to footnote 38" id="Backf00038">38</a>) or Article 3 (“act as insolvency practitioner”) of the Insolvency (Northern Ireland) Order 1989(<a class="LegFootnoteRef" href="#f00039" title="Go to footnote 39" id="Backf00039">39</a>).&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(12) In <a href="#regulation-35-1" title="Go to reg. 35(1)">paragraph (1)</a>, “<span class="LegTerm" id="term-letting-agency-work">letting agency work</span>” means work—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-35-12-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">consisting of things done in response to instructions received from—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4No" id="regulation-35-12-a-i">(i)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4Text">a person (a “prospective landlord”) seeking to find another person to whom to let land for a term of a month or more, or</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4No" id="regulation-35-12-a-ii">(ii)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4Text">a person (a “prospective tenant”) seeking to find land to rent for a term of a month or more, and</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-35-12-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">done—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4No" id="regulation-35-12-b-i">(i)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4Text">in relation to a prospective landlord, from the point that the prospective landlord instructs P, or</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4No" id="regulation-35-12-b-ii">(ii)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4Text">otherwise in the course of concluding an agreement for the letting of land for a term of a month or more.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(13) For the purposes of <a href="#regulation-35-12" title="Go to reg. 35(12)">paragraph (12)</a>—&#xD;</p><a class="LegAnchorID" id="p02816"></a><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3">“<span class="LegTerm" id="term-land">land</span>” includes part of a building and part of any other structure;&#xD;</p><a class="LegAnchorID" id="p02820"></a><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3">“<span class="LegTerm" id="term-letting-agency-work">letting agency work</span>” does not include the things listed in <a href="#regulation-35-14" title="Go to reg. 35(14)">paragraph (14)</a> when done by, or by employees of, a firm or sole practitioner if neither the firm or sole practitioner, nor any of their employees, does anything else within <a href="#regulation-35-12" title="Go to reg. 35(12)">paragraph (12)</a>.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(14) Those things are—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-35-14-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">publishing advertisements or disseminating information;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-35-14-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">providing a means by which a prospective landlord or a prospective tenant can, in response to an advertisement or dissemination of information, make direct contact with a prospective tenant or a prospective landlord;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-35-14-c">(c)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">providing a means by which a prospective landlord and a prospective tenant can communicate directly with each other;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-35-14-d">(d)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">the provision of legal or notarial services by a barrister, advocate, solicitor or other legal representative communications with whom may be the subject of a claim to professional privilege or, in Scotland, protected from disclosure in legal proceedings on grounds of confidentiality of communications.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(15) <a href="#regulation-35-1" title="Go to reg. 35(1)">Paragraph (1)</a><a href="#regulation-35-1-a" title="Go to reg. 35(1)(a)">(a)</a> and <a href="#regulation-35-1-b" title="Go to reg. 35(1)(b)">(b)</a> are to be read with <a class="LegCitation" title="Go to item of legislation" rel="cite" href="https://www.legislation.gov.uk/ukpga/2000/8/section/22">section 22</a> (regulated activities) of the <a class="LegCitation" title="Go to item of legislation" rel="cite" href="https://www.legislation.gov.uk/ukpga/2000/8/contents">Financial Services and Markets Act 2000</a>, any relevant order under <a class="LegCitation" title="Go to item of legislation" rel="cite" href="https://www.legislation.gov.uk/ukpga/2000/8/section/22">that section</a> and <a class="LegCitation" title="Go to item of legislation" rel="cite" href="https://www.legislation.gov.uk/ukpga/2000/8/schedule/2">Schedule 2</a> (regulated activities) to <a class="LegCitation" title="Go to item of legislation" rel="cite" href="https://www.legislation.gov.uk/ukpga/2000/8/contents">that Act</a>.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(16) For the purposes of <a href="#regulation-33" title="Go to reg. 33">regulation 33</a><a href="#regulation-33-1" title="Go to reg. 33(1)">(1)</a> (finance: reporting obligations), information or another matter comes to a relevant firm “in the course of carrying on its business” if the information or other matter comes to the firm—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-35-16-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">in the case of a relevant firm within <a href="#regulation-35-1" title="Go to reg. 35(1)">paragraph (1)</a><a href="#regulation-35-1-a" title="Go to reg. 35(1)(a)">(a)</a>, in the course of carrying on an activity in respect of which the permission mentioned in that provision is required;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-35-16-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">in the case of a relevant firm within <a href="#regulation-35-1" title="Go to reg. 35(1)">paragraph (1)</a><a href="#regulation-35-1-c" title="Go to reg. 35(1)(c)">(c)</a><a href="#regulation-35-1-c-i" title="Go to reg. 35(1)(c)(i)">(i)</a>, in the course of carrying out statutory audit work within the meaning of <a class="LegCitation" title="Go to item of legislation" rel="cite" href="https://www.legislation.gov.uk/ukpga/2006/46/section/1210">section 1210</a> (meaning of statutory auditor) of the <a class="LegCitation" title="Go to item of legislation" rel="cite" href="https://www.legislation.gov.uk/ukpga/2006/46/contents">Companies Act 2006</a>(<a class="LegFootnoteRef" href="#f00040" title="Go to footnote 40" id="Backf00040">40</a>);</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-35-16-c">(c)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">in the case of a relevant firm within <a href="#regulation-35-1" title="Go to reg. 35(1)">paragraph (1)</a><a href="#regulation-35-1-c" title="Go to reg. 35(1)(c)">(c)</a><a href="#regulation-35-1-c-ii" title="Go to reg. 35(1)(c)(ii)">(ii)</a>, in the course of carrying out an audit required by the <a class="LegCitation" title="Go to item of legislation" rel="cite" href="https://www.legislation.gov.uk/ukpga/2014/2/contents">Local Audit and Accountability Act 2014</a>;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-35-16-d">(d)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">in the case of a relevant firm within <a href="#regulation-35-1" title="Go to reg. 35(1)">paragraph (1)</a><a href="#regulation-35-1-f" title="Go to reg. 35(1)(f)">(f)</a>, in the course of carrying on an activity in respect of which the licence mentioned in that provision is required;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-35-16-e">(e)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">in the case of a relevant firm within <a href="#regulation-35-1" title="Go to reg. 35(1)">paragraph (1)</a><a href="#regulation-35-1-k" title="Go to reg. 35(1)(k)">(k)</a>—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4No" id="regulation-35-16-e-i">(i)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4Text">in the course of trading, or acting as an intermediary in the sale or purchase of, works of art when the value of the transaction, or a series of linked transactions, amounts to £10,000 or more, or</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4No" id="regulation-35-16-e-ii">(ii)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4Text">in the course of storing works of art where the value of the works of art so stored for a person amounts to £10,000 or more;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-35-16-f">(f)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">in the case of a relevant firm within any other provision of <a href="#regulation-35-1" title="Go to reg. 35(1)">paragraph (1)</a>, in the course of carrying on an activity mentioned in that provision.</span>&#xD;</p><div class="LegAnnotations"><p class="LegAnnotationsGroupHeading">Commencement Information</p><div class="LegCommentaryItem" id="commentary-key-6d4f709d884030657de8edea8f57e7eb"><p class="LegCommentaryPara"><span class="LegCommentaryType">I35</span><span class="LegCommentaryText">Reg. 35 in force at 15.10.2026, see <a class="LegCitation" href="/id/uksi/2026/1071/regulation/1/2" title="Go to reg. 1(2)" rel="cite"><strong>reg. 1(2)</strong></a></span></p></div></div><h3 class="LegP1GroupTitle">Finance: powers to request information<span class="LegExtentRestriction" title="Applies to "><span class="btr"></span>U.K.<span class="bbl"></span><span class="bbr"></span></span></h3><p class="LegP1ParaText"><span class="LegP1No" id="regulation-36">36.</span>—(1) The Treasury may request a designated person to provide information about—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-36-1-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">funds or economic resources owned, held or controlled by or on behalf of the designated person, or</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-36-1-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">any disposal of such funds or economic resources.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(2) The Treasury may request a designated person to provide such information as the Treasury may reasonably require about expenditure—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-36-2-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">by the designated person, or</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-36-2-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">for the benefit of the designated person.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(3) For the purposes of <a href="#regulation-36-2" title="Go to reg. 36(2)">paragraph (2)</a> expenditure for the benefit of a designated person includes expenditure on the discharge (or partial discharge) of a financial obligation for which the designated person is wholly or partly responsible.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(4) The power in <a href="#regulation-36-1" title="Go to reg. 36(1)">paragraph (1)</a> or <a href="#regulation-36-2" title="Go to reg. 36(2)">(2)</a> is exercisable only where the Treasury believes that it is necessary for the purpose of monitoring compliance with or detecting evasion of any provision of <a href="#part-3" title="Go to Pt. 3">Part 3</a> (Finance).&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(5) The Treasury may request a person acting under a Treasury licence to provide information about—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-36-5-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">funds or economic resources dealt with under the licence, or</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-36-5-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">funds or economic resources made available under the licence.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(6) The Treasury may request a person to provide information within <a href="#regulation-36-7" title="Go to reg. 36(7)">paragraph (7)</a> if the Treasury believes that the person may be able to provide the information.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(7) Information within <a href="#regulation-36-7" title="Go to reg. 36(7)">this paragraph</a> is such information as the Treasury may reasonably require for the purpose of—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-36-7-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">establishing for the purposes of any provision of <a href="#part-3" title="Go to Pt. 3">Part 3</a> (Finance)—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4No" id="regulation-36-7-a-i">(i)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4Text">the nature and amount or quantity of any funds or economic resources owned, held or controlled by or on behalf of a designated person,</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4No" id="regulation-36-7-a-ii">(ii)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4Text">the nature and amount or quantity of any funds or economic resources made available directly or indirectly to, or for the benefit of, a designated person, or</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4No" id="regulation-36-7-a-iii">(iii)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4Text">the nature of any financial transactions entered into by a designated person;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-36-7-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">monitoring compliance with or detecting evasion of—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4No" id="regulation-36-7-b-i">(i)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4Text">any provision of <a href="#part-3" title="Go to Pt. 3">Part 3</a>,</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4No" id="regulation-36-7-b-ii">(ii)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4Text"><a href="#regulation-33" title="Go to reg. 33">regulation 33</a> (finance: reporting obligations), or</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4No" id="regulation-36-7-b-iii">(iii)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4Text">any condition of a Treasury licence;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-36-7-c">(c)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">detecting or obtaining evidence of the commission of an offence under <a href="#part-3" title="Go to Pt. 3">Part 3</a> or <a href="#regulation-30" title="Go to reg. 30">regulation 30</a> (finance: licensing offences) or <a href="#regulation-33" title="Go to reg. 33">33</a>.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(8) The Treasury may specify the way in which, and the period within which, information is to be provided.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(9) If no such period is specified, the information which has been requested must be provided within a reasonable time.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(10) A request may include a continuing obligation to keep the Treasury informed as circumstances change, or on such regular basis as the Treasury may specify.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(11) Information requested under <a href="#regulation-36" title="Go to reg. 36">this regulation</a> may relate to any period of time during which a person is, or was, a designated person.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(12) Information requested by virtue of <a href="#regulation-36-1" title="Go to reg. 36(1)">paragraph (1)</a><a href="#regulation-36-1-b" title="Go to reg. 36(1)(b)">(b)</a>, <a href="#regulation-36-2" title="Go to reg. 36(2)">(2)</a>, or <a href="#regulation-36-7" title="Go to reg. 36(7)">(7)</a><a href="#regulation-36-7-a" title="Go to reg. 36(7)(a)">(a)</a><a href="#regulation-36-7-a-iii" title="Go to reg. 36(7)(a)(iii)">(iii)</a> may relate to any period before a person became a designated person (as well as, or instead of, any subsequent period).&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(13) Expressions used in <a href="#regulation-36" title="Go to reg. 36">this regulation</a> have the same meaning as they have in <a href="#part-3" title="Go to Pt. 3">Part 3</a>.&#xD;</p><div class="LegAnnotations"><p class="LegAnnotationsGroupHeading">Commencement Information</p><div class="LegCommentaryItem" id="commentary-key-f12cf4462ece866fd01f7866d5aaa8c0"><p class="LegCommentaryPara"><span class="LegCommentaryType">I36</span><span class="LegCommentaryText">Reg. 36 in force at 15.10.2026, see <a class="LegCitation" href="/id/uksi/2026/1071/regulation/1/2" title="Go to reg. 1(2)" rel="cite"><strong>reg. 1(2)</strong></a></span></p></div></div><h3 class="LegP1GroupTitle">Finance: production of documents<span class="LegExtentRestriction" title="Applies to "><span class="btr"></span>U.K.<span class="bbl"></span><span class="bbr"></span></span></h3><p class="LegP1ParaText"><span class="LegP1No" id="regulation-37">37.</span>—(1) A request under <a href="#regulation-36" title="Go to reg. 36">regulation 36</a> (finance: powers to request information) may include a request to produce specified documents or documents of a specified description.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(2) Where the Treasury requests that documents be produced, the Treasury may—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-37-2-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">take copies of or extracts from any document so produced,</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-37-2-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">request any person producing a document to give an explanation of it, and</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-37-2-c">(c)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">where that person is a body corporate, partnership or unincorporated body other than a partnership, request any person who is—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4No" id="regulation-37-2-c-i">(i)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4Text">in the case of a partnership, a present or past partner or employee of the partnership, or</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4No" id="regulation-37-2-c-ii">(ii)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4Text">in any other case, a present or past officer or employee of the body concerned,</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegRHS LegP3Text">to give such an explanation.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(3) Where the Treasury requests a designated person or a person acting under a Treasury licence to produce documents, that person must—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-37-3-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">take reasonable steps to obtain the documents (if they are not already in the person's possession or control);</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-37-3-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">keep the documents under the person's possession or control (except for the purpose of providing them to the Treasury or as the Treasury may otherwise permit).</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(4) In <a href="#regulation-37" title="Go to reg. 37">this regulation</a>, “<span class="LegTerm" id="term-designated-person">designated person</span>” has the same meaning as it has in <a href="#part-3" title="Go to Pt. 3">Part 3</a> (Finance).&#xD;</p><div class="LegAnnotations"><p class="LegAnnotationsGroupHeading">Commencement Information</p><div class="LegCommentaryItem" id="commentary-key-424f789b284e40720c96827625da8458"><p class="LegCommentaryPara"><span class="LegCommentaryType">I37</span><span class="LegCommentaryText">Reg. 37 in force at 15.10.2026, see <a class="LegCitation" href="/id/uksi/2026/1071/regulation/1/2" title="Go to reg. 1(2)" rel="cite"><strong>reg. 1(2)</strong></a></span></p></div></div><h3 class="LegP1GroupTitle">Finance: information offences<span class="LegExtentRestriction" title="Applies to "><span class="btr"></span>U.K.<span class="bbl"></span><span class="bbr"></span></span></h3><p class="LegP1ParaText"><span class="LegP1No" id="regulation-38">38.</span>—(1) A person commits an offence, if that person—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-38-1-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">without reasonable excuse, refuses or fails within the time and in the manner specified (or, if no time has been specified, within a reasonable time) to comply with any request under <a href="#regulation-36" title="Go to reg. 36">regulation 36</a> (finance: powers to request information);</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-38-1-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">knowingly or recklessly gives any information, or produces any document, which is false in a material particular in response to such a request;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-38-1-c">(c)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">with intent to evade any provision of <a href="#regulation-36" title="Go to reg. 36">regulation 36</a> or <a href="#regulation-37" title="Go to reg. 37">37</a> (finance: production of documents), destroys, mutilates, defaces, conceals or removes any document;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-38-1-d">(d)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">otherwise intentionally obstructs the Treasury in the exercise of their powers under <a href="#regulation-36" title="Go to reg. 36">regulation 36</a> or <a href="#regulation-37" title="Go to reg. 37">37</a>.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(2) Where a person is convicted of an offence under <a href="#regulation-38" title="Go to reg. 38">this regulation</a>, the court may make an order requiring that person, within such period as may be specified in the order, to comply with the request.&#xD;</p><div class="LegAnnotations"><p class="LegAnnotationsGroupHeading">Commencement Information</p><div class="LegCommentaryItem" id="commentary-key-b7fd2eb0bbab15517be2ffc3aa759d1b"><p class="LegCommentaryPara"><span class="LegCommentaryType">I38</span><span class="LegCommentaryText">Reg. 38 in force at 15.10.2026, see <a class="LegCitation" href="/id/uksi/2026/1071/regulation/1/2" title="Go to reg. 1(2)" rel="cite"><strong>reg. 1(2)</strong></a></span></p></div></div><h3 class="LegP1GroupTitle">Disclosure of information<span class="LegExtentRestriction" title="Applies to "><span class="btr"></span>U.K.<span class="bbl"></span><span class="bbr"></span></span></h3><p class="LegP1ParaText"><span class="LegP1No" id="regulation-39">39.</span>—(1) The Secretary of State or the Treasury may, in accordance with <a href="#regulation-39" title="Go to reg. 39">this regulation</a>, disclose—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-39-1-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">any information obtained under or by virtue of <a href="#part-6" title="Go to Pt. 6">Part 6</a> (Exceptions and licences) or <a href="#part-7" title="Go to Pt. 7">this Part</a>, or</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-39-1-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">any information held in connection with—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4No" id="regulation-39-1-b-i">(i)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4Text">anything done under or by virtue of <a href="#part-2" title="Go to Pt. 2">Part 2</a> (Designation of persons), <a href="#part-3" title="Go to Pt. 3">Part 3</a> (Finance) or <a href="#part-4" title="Go to Pt. 4">Part 4</a> (Director disqualification sanctions), or</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4No" id="regulation-39-1-b-ii">(ii)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4Text">any exception or licence under <a href="#part-6" title="Go to Pt. 6">Part 6</a> or anything done in accordance with such an exception or under the authority of such a licence.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(2) Information referred to in <a href="#regulation-39-1" title="Go to reg. 39(1)">paragraph (1)</a> may be disclosed for, or in connection with, any of the following purposes—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-39-2-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">any purpose stated in <a href="#regulation-4" title="Go to reg. 4">regulation 4</a> (purposes);</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-39-2-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">the exercise of functions under these Regulations;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-39-2-c">(c)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">facilitating, monitoring or ensuring compliance with these Regulations;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-39-2-d">(d)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">taking any action with a view to instituting, or otherwise for the purposes of, any proceedings in the United Kingdom—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4No" id="regulation-39-2-d-i">(i)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4Text">for an offence under any provision of these Regulations, or</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4No" id="regulation-39-2-d-ii">(ii)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4Text">in relation to a monetary penalty under <a class="LegCitation" title="Go to item of legislation" rel="cite" href="https://www.legislation.gov.uk/ukpga/2017/3/section/146">section 146</a> (breach of financial sanctions legislation) of the <a class="LegCitation" title="Go to item of legislation" rel="cite" href="https://www.legislation.gov.uk/ukpga/2017/3/contents">Policing and Crime Act 2017</a>(<a class="LegFootnoteRef" href="#f00041" title="Go to footnote 41" id="Backf00041">41</a>);</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-39-2-e">(e)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">taking any action with a view to instituting, or otherwise for the purposes of, any proceedings in any of the Channel Islands, the Isle of Man or any British overseas territory for an offence under a provision in any such jurisdiction that is similar to a provision of these Regulations;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-39-2-f">(f)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">compliance with an international obligation(<a class="LegFootnoteRef" href="#f00042" title="Go to footnote 42" id="Backf00042">42</a>);</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-39-2-g">(g)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">facilitating the exercise by an authority outside the United Kingdom or by an international organisation of functions which correspond to functions under these Regulations.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(3) Information referred to in <a href="#regulation-39-1" title="Go to reg. 39(1)">paragraph (1)</a> may be disclosed to the following persons—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-39-3-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">a police officer;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-39-3-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">any person holding or acting in any office under or in the service of—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4No" id="regulation-39-3-b-i">(i)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4Text">the Crown in right of the Government of the United Kingdom,</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4No" id="regulation-39-3-b-ii">(ii)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4Text">the Crown in right of the Scottish Government, the Northern Ireland Executive or the Welsh Government,</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4No" id="regulation-39-3-b-iii">(iii)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4Text">the States of Jersey, Guernsey or Alderney or the Chief Pleas of Sark,</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4No" id="regulation-39-3-b-iv">(iv)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4Text">the Government of the Isle of Man, or</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4No" id="regulation-39-3-b-v">(v)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4Text">the Government of any British overseas territory;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-39-3-c">(c)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">any law officer of the Crown for Jersey, Guernsey or the Isle of Man;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-39-3-d">(d)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">the Scottish Legal Aid Board;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-39-3-e">(e)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">the Financial Conduct Authority, the Prudential Regulation Authority, the Bank of England, the Jersey Financial Services Commission, the Guernsey Financial Services Commission or the Isle of Man Financial Services Authority;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-39-3-f">(f)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">any other regulatory body (whether or not in the United Kingdom);</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-39-3-g">(g)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">any organ of the United Nations;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-39-3-h">(h)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">the Council of the European Union, the European Commission or the European External Action Service;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-39-3-i">(i)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">the Government of any country;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-39-3-j">(j)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">any other person where the Secretary of State or the Treasury (as the case may be) consider that it is appropriate to disclose the information.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(4) Information referred to in <a href="#regulation-39-1" title="Go to reg. 39(1)">paragraph (1)</a> may be disclosed to any person with the consent of a person who, in their own right, is entitled to the information.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(5) In <a href="#regulation-39-4" title="Go to reg. 39(4)">paragraph (4)</a>, “<span class="LegTerm" id="term-in-their-own-right">in their own right</span>” means not merely in the capacity as a servant or agent of another person.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(6) In <a href="#regulation-39-1" title="Go to reg. 39(1)">paragraph (1)</a><a href="#regulation-39-1-b" title="Go to reg. 39(1)(b)">(b)</a>, the reference to information includes information obtained at a time when any provision of these Regulations is not in force.&#xD;</p><div class="LegAnnotations"><p class="LegAnnotationsGroupHeading">Commencement Information</p><div class="LegCommentaryItem" id="commentary-key-622c07879ee46822134fe92ace721a8f"><p class="LegCommentaryPara"><span class="LegCommentaryType">I39</span><span class="LegCommentaryText">Reg. 39 in force at 15.10.2026, see <a class="LegCitation" href="/id/uksi/2026/1071/regulation/1/2" title="Go to reg. 1(2)" rel="cite"><strong>reg. 1(2)</strong></a></span></p></div></div><h3 class="LegP1GroupTitle">Finance: disclosure to the Treasury<span class="LegExtentRestriction" title="Applies to "><span class="btr"></span>U.K.<span class="bbl"></span><span class="bbr"></span></span></h3><p class="LegP1ParaText"><span class="LegP1No" id="regulation-40">40.</span>—(1) A relevant public authority may disclose information to the Treasury if the disclosure is made for the purpose of enabling or assisting the Treasury to discharge any of its functions in connection with any sanctions regulations(<a class="LegFootnoteRef" href="#f00043" title="Go to footnote 43" id="Backf00043">43</a>) contained in these Regulations.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(2) In <a href="#regulation-40" title="Go to reg. 40">this regulation</a>—&#xD;</p><a class="LegAnchorID" id="p03336"></a><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3">“<span class="LegTerm" id="term-relevant-public-authority">relevant public authority</span>” means—&#xD;</p><div><div class="LegAlphaList"><div class="LegListItem" id="p03339"><div class="LegLevel4No LegListItemNo">(a)</div><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel4">any person holding or acting in any office under or in the service of—&#xD;</p><div><div class="LegRomanList"><div class="LegListItem" id="p03342"><div class="LegLevel5No LegListItemNo">(i)</div><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel5">the Crown in right of the Government of the United Kingdom;&#xD;</p></div><div class="LegListItem" id="p03346"><div class="LegLevel5No LegListItemNo">(ii)</div><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel5">the Crown in right of the Scottish Government, the Northern Ireland Executive or the Welsh Government;&#xD;</p></div></div></div></div><div class="LegListItem" id="p03351"><div class="LegLevel4No LegListItemNo">(b)</div><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel4">any local authority;&#xD;</p></div><div class="LegListItem" id="p03355"><div class="LegLevel4No LegListItemNo">(c)</div><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel4">any police officer;&#xD;</p></div><div class="LegListItem" id="p03359"><div class="LegLevel4No LegListItemNo">(d)</div><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel4">the Financial Conduct Authority, the Prudential Regulation Authority, the Bank of England or any other regulatory body in the United Kingdom;&#xD;</p></div><div class="LegListItem" id="p03363"><div class="LegLevel4No LegListItemNo">(e)</div><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel4">any other person exercising functions of a public nature;&#xD;</p></div></div></div><a class="LegAnchorID" id="p03368"></a><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3">“<span class="LegTerm" id="term-local-authority">local authority</span>” means—&#xD;</p><div><div class="LegAlphaList"><div class="LegListItem" id="p03371"><div class="LegLevel4No LegListItemNo">(a)</div><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel4">in relation to England—&#xD;</p><div><div class="LegRomanList"><div class="LegListItem" id="p03374"><div class="LegLevel5No LegListItemNo">(i)</div><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel5">a county council;&#xD;</p></div><div class="LegListItem" id="p03378"><div class="LegLevel5No LegListItemNo">(ii)</div><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel5">a district council;&#xD;</p></div><div class="LegListItem" id="p03382"><div class="LegLevel5No LegListItemNo">(iii)</div><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel5">a London Borough council;&#xD;</p></div><div class="LegListItem" id="p03386"><div class="LegLevel5No LegListItemNo">(iv)</div><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel5">the Common Council of the City of London in its capacity as a local authority;&#xD;</p></div><div class="LegListItem" id="p03390"><div class="LegLevel5No LegListItemNo">(v)</div><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel5">the Council of the Isles of Scilly;&#xD;</p></div><div class="LegListItem" id="p03394"><div class="LegLevel5No LegListItemNo">(vi)</div><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel5">an eligible parish council within the meaning of <a class="LegCitation" title="Go to item of legislation" rel="cite" href="https://www.legislation.gov.uk/ukpga/2000/22/section/1">section 1</a> <a class="LegCitation" title="Go to item of legislation" rel="cite" href="https://www.legislation.gov.uk/ukpga/2000/22/section/1">(2)</a> (meaning of “<span class="LegTerm" id="term-local-authority">local authority</span>” in Part I) of the <a class="LegCitation" title="Go to item of legislation" rel="cite" href="https://www.legislation.gov.uk/ukpga/2000/22/contents">Local Government Act 2000</a>(<a class="LegFootnoteRef" href="#f00044" title="Go to footnote 44" id="Backf00044">44</a>);&#xD;</p></div></div></div></div><div class="LegListItem" id="p03406"><div class="LegLevel4No LegListItemNo">(b)</div><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel4">in relation to Wales, a county council, a county borough council or a community council;&#xD;</p></div><div class="LegListItem" id="p03410"><div class="LegLevel4No LegListItemNo">(c)</div><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel4">in relation to Scotland, a council constituted under <a class="LegCitation" title="Go to item of legislation" rel="cite" href="https://www.legislation.gov.uk/ukpga/1994/39/section/2">section 2</a> (constitution of councils) of the <a class="LegCitation" title="Go to item of legislation" rel="cite" href="https://www.legislation.gov.uk/ukpga/1994/39/contents">Local Government etc. (Scotland) Act 1994</a>(<a class="LegFootnoteRef" href="#f00045" title="Go to footnote 45" id="Backf00045">45</a>);&#xD;</p></div><div class="LegListItem" id="p03424"><div class="LegLevel4No LegListItemNo">(d)</div><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel4">in relation to Northern Ireland, a district council.&#xD;</p></div></div></div><div class="LegAnnotations"><p class="LegAnnotationsGroupHeading">Commencement Information</p><div class="LegCommentaryItem" id="commentary-key-3bd68dd92408a9f61bddaa5a1deaad8d"><p class="LegCommentaryPara"><span class="LegCommentaryType">I40</span><span class="LegCommentaryText">Reg. 40 in force at 15.10.2026, see <a class="LegCitation" href="/id/uksi/2026/1071/regulation/1/2" title="Go to reg. 1(2)" rel="cite"><strong>reg. 1(2)</strong></a></span></p></div></div><h3 class="LegP1GroupTitle"> <a href="#part-7" title="Go to Pt. 7">Part 7</a>: supplementary<span class="LegExtentRestriction" title="Applies to "><span class="btr"></span>U.K.<span class="bbl"></span><span class="bbr"></span></span></h3><p class="LegP1ParaText"><span class="LegP1No" id="regulation-41">41.</span>—(1) A disclosure of information under <a href="#regulation-39" title="Go to reg. 39">regulation 39</a> (disclosure of information) or <a href="#regulation-40" title="Go to reg. 40">40</a> (finance: disclosure to the Treasury) does not breach any restriction on such disclosure imposed by statute or otherwise.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(2) But nothing in those regulations authorises a disclosure that—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-41-2-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">contravenes the data protection legislation, or</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-41-2-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">is prohibited by any of <a class="LegCitation" title="Go to item of legislation" rel="cite" href="https://www.legislation.gov.uk/ukpga/2016/25/part/1">Parts 1</a> (General privacy protections) to <a class="LegCitation" title="Go to item of legislation" rel="cite" href="https://www.legislation.gov.uk/ukpga/2016/25/part/7">7</a> (Bulk personal dataset warrants) or <a class="LegCitation" title="Go to item of legislation" rel="cite" href="https://www.legislation.gov.uk/ukpga/2016/25/part/9/chapter/1">Chapter 1</a> of <a class="LegCitation" title="Go to item of legislation" rel="cite" href="https://www.legislation.gov.uk/ukpga/2016/25/part/9">Part 9</a> (Miscellaneous and general provisions) of the <a class="LegCitation" title="Go to item of legislation" rel="cite" href="https://www.legislation.gov.uk/ukpga/2016/25/contents">Investigatory Powers Act 2016</a>(<a class="LegFootnoteRef" href="#f00046" title="Go to footnote 46" id="Backf00046">46</a>).</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(3) Nothing in <a href="#part-7" title="Go to Pt. 7">this Part</a> is to be read as requiring a person who has acted or is acting as counsel or solicitor for any person to disclose any privileged information in their possession in that capacity.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(4) <a href="#regulation-39" title="Go to reg. 39">Regulations 39</a> and <a href="#regulation-40" title="Go to reg. 40">40</a> do not limit the circumstances in which information may be disclosed apart from those regulations.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(5) Nothing in <a href="#part-7" title="Go to Pt. 7">this Part</a> limits any conditions which may be contained in a Treasury licence.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(6) In <a href="#regulation-41" title="Go to reg. 41">this regulation</a>—&#xD;</p><a class="LegAnchorID" id="p03509"></a><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3">“<span class="LegTerm" id="term-the-data-protection-legislation">the data protection legislation</span>” has the same meaning as in the <a class="LegCitation" title="Go to item of legislation" rel="cite" href="https://www.legislation.gov.uk/ukpga/2018/12/contents">Data Protection Act 2018</a> (see section 3 (terms relating to the processing of personal data) of that Act)(<a class="LegFootnoteRef" href="#f00047" title="Go to footnote 47" id="Backf00047">47</a>);&#xD;</p><a class="LegAnchorID" id="p03517"></a><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3">“<span class="LegTerm" id="term-privileged-information">privileged information</span>” means information with respect to which a claim to legal professional privilege (in Scotland, to confidentiality of communications) could be maintained in legal proceedings.&#xD;</p><div class="LegAnnotations"><p class="LegAnnotationsGroupHeading">Commencement Information</p><div class="LegCommentaryItem" id="commentary-key-5f0f2e1fe6f3c97d8c635728ecfc214e"><p class="LegCommentaryPara"><span class="LegCommentaryType">I41</span><span class="LegCommentaryText">Reg. 41 in force at 15.10.2026, see <a class="LegCitation" href="/id/uksi/2026/1071/regulation/1/2" title="Go to reg. 1(2)" rel="cite"><strong>reg. 1(2)</strong></a></span></p></div></div><a class="LegAnchorID" id="part-8"></a><div class="LegClearPart"></div><h2 class="LegPart"><span class="LegPartNo">Part 8<span class="LegExtentRestriction" title="Applies to "><span class="btr"></span>U.K.<span class="bbl"></span><span class="bbr"></span></span></span><span class="LegPartTitle">Enforcement</span></h2><h3 class="LegP1GroupTitleFirst">Penalties for offences<span class="LegExtentRestriction" title="Applies to "><span class="btr"></span>U.K.<span class="bbl"></span><span class="bbr"></span></span></h3><p class="LegP1ParaText"><span class="LegP1No" id="regulation-42">42.</span>—(1) A person who commits an offence under any provision of <a href="#part-3" title="Go to Pt. 3">Part 3</a> (Finance) or <a href="#regulation-30" title="Go to reg. 30">regulation 30</a> (finance: licensing offences) is liable—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-42-1-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">on summary conviction in England and Wales, to imprisonment for a term not exceeding the general limit in a magistrates’ court or a fine (or both);</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-42-1-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">on summary conviction in Scotland, to imprisonment for a term not exceeding 12 months or a fine not exceeding the statutory maximum (or both);</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-42-1-c">(c)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">on summary conviction in Northern Ireland, to imprisonment for a term not exceeding 6 months or a fine not exceeding the statutory maximum (or both);</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-42-1-d">(d)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">on conviction on indictment, to imprisonment for a term not exceeding 7 years or a fine (or both).</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(2) A person who commits an offence under <a href="#regulation-12" title="Go to reg. 12">regulation 12</a><a href="#regulation-12-6" title="Go to reg. 12(6)">(6)</a> (confidential information in certain cases where designation power used) or <a href="#regulation-31" title="Go to reg. 31">31</a> (director disqualification: licensing offences) is liable—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-42-2-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">on summary conviction in England and Wales, to imprisonment for a term not exceeding the general limit in a magistrates’ court or a fine (or both);</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-42-2-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">on summary conviction in Scotland, to imprisonment for a term not exceeding 12 months or a fine not exceeding the statutory maximum (or both);</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-42-2-c">(c)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">on summary conviction in Northern Ireland, to imprisonment for a term not exceeding 6 months or a fine not exceeding the statutory maximum (or both);</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-42-2-d">(d)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">on conviction on indictment, to imprisonment for a term not exceeding 2 years or a fine (or both).</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(3) A person who commits an offence under <a href="#regulation-33" title="Go to reg. 33">regulation 33</a><a href="#regulation-33-9" title="Go to reg. 33(9)">(9)</a> or (10) (finance: reporting obligations) or regulation <a href="#regulation-38" title="Go to reg. 38">38</a> (finance: information offences) is liable—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-42-3-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">on summary conviction in England and Wales, to imprisonment for a term not exceeding 6 months or a fine (or both);</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-42-3-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">on summary conviction in Scotland, to imprisonment for a term not exceeding 6 months or a fine not exceeding level 5 on the standard scale (or both);</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-42-3-c">(c)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">on summary conviction in Northern Ireland, to imprisonment for a term not exceeding 6 months or a fine not exceeding level 5 on the standard scale (or both).</span>&#xD;</p><div class="LegAnnotations"><p class="LegAnnotationsGroupHeading">Commencement Information</p><div class="LegCommentaryItem" id="commentary-key-0a4ae60c02f01d720708527b5de06277"><p class="LegCommentaryPara"><span class="LegCommentaryType">I42</span><span class="LegCommentaryText">Reg. 42 in force at 15.10.2026, see <a class="LegCitation" href="/id/uksi/2026/1071/regulation/1/2" title="Go to reg. 1(2)" rel="cite"><strong>reg. 1(2)</strong></a></span></p></div></div><h3 class="LegP1GroupTitle">Liability of officers of bodies corporate etc.<span class="LegExtentRestriction" title="Applies to "><span class="btr"></span>U.K.<span class="bbl"></span><span class="bbr"></span></span></h3><p class="LegP1ParaText"><span class="LegP1No" id="regulation-43">43.</span>—(1) Where an offence under these Regulations, committed by a body corporate—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-43-1-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">is committed with the consent or connivance of any director, manager, secretary or other similar officer of the body corporate, or any person who was purporting to act in any such capacity, or</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-43-1-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">is attributable to any neglect on the part of any such person,</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2Text">that person as well as the body corporate is guilty of the offence and is liable to be proceeded against and punished accordingly.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(2) In <a href="#regulation-41-1" title="Go to reg. 41(1)">paragraph (1)</a>, “<span class="LegTerm" id="term-director">director</span>”, in relation to a body corporate whose affairs are managed by its members, means a member of the body corporate.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(3) <a href="#regulation-41-1" title="Go to reg. 41(1)">Paragraph (1)</a> also applies in relation to a body that is not a body corporate, with the substitution for the reference to a director of the body of a reference—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-43-3-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">in the case of a partnership, to a partner;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-43-3-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">in the case of an unincorporated body other than a partnership—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4No" id="regulation-43-3-b-i">(i)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4Text">where the body’s affairs are managed by its members, to a member of the body;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4No" id="regulation-43-3-b-ii">(ii)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4Text">in any other case, to a member of the governing body.</span>&#xD;</p><div class="LegAnnotations"><p class="LegAnnotationsGroupHeading">Commencement Information</p><div class="LegCommentaryItem" id="commentary-key-02042055cf0fce52701f4a426c55b95d"><p class="LegCommentaryPara"><span class="LegCommentaryType">I43</span><span class="LegCommentaryText">Reg. 43 in force at 15.10.2026, see <a class="LegCitation" href="/id/uksi/2026/1071/regulation/1/2" title="Go to reg. 1(2)" rel="cite"><strong>reg. 1(2)</strong></a></span></p></div></div><h3 class="LegP1GroupTitle">Jurisdiction to try offences<span class="LegExtentRestriction" title="Applies to "><span class="btr"></span>U.K.<span class="bbl"></span><span class="bbr"></span></span></h3><p class="LegP1ParaText"><span class="LegP1No" id="regulation-44">44.</span>—(1) Wherever an offence under these Regulations is committed (whether in the United Kingdom or outside the United Kingdom)—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-44-1-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">proceedings for the offence may be taken at any place in the United Kingdom, and</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-44-1-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">the offence may for all incidental purposes be treated as having been committed at any such place.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(2) In the application of <a href="#regulation-42-1" title="Go to reg. 42(1)">paragraph (1)</a> to Scotland, any such proceedings against a person may be taken—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-44-2-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">in any sheriff court district in which the person is apprehended or is in custody, or</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-44-2-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">in such sheriff court district as the Lord Advocate may determine.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(3) In <a href="#regulation-42-2" title="Go to reg. 42(2)">paragraph (2)</a>, “<span class="LegTerm" id="term-sheriff-court-district">sheriff court district</span>” is to be read in accordance with the Criminal Procedure (Scotland) Act 1995 (see section 307(1) (interpretation) of that Act)(<a class="LegFootnoteRef" href="#f00048" title="Go to footnote 48" id="Backf00048">48</a>).&#xD;</p><div class="LegAnnotations"><p class="LegAnnotationsGroupHeading">Commencement Information</p><div class="LegCommentaryItem" id="commentary-key-2e322281fa72ee00e0e948cf4aa482c1"><p class="LegCommentaryPara"><span class="LegCommentaryType">I44</span><span class="LegCommentaryText">Reg. 44 in force at 15.10.2026, see <a class="LegCitation" href="/id/uksi/2026/1071/regulation/1/2" title="Go to reg. 1(2)" rel="cite"><strong>reg. 1(2)</strong></a></span></p></div></div><h3 class="LegP1GroupTitle">Procedure for offences by unincorporated bodies<span class="LegExtentRestriction"
