title="Applies to "><span class="btr"></span>U.K.<span class="bbl"></span><span class="bbr"></span></span></h3><p class="LegP1ParaText"><span class="LegP1No" id="regulation-45">45.</span>—(1) <a href="#regulation-43-2" title="Go to reg. 43(2)">Paragraphs (2)</a> and <a href="#regulation-43-3" title="Go to reg. 43(3)">(3)</a> apply if it is alleged that an offence under these Regulations has been committed by an unincorporated body (as opposed to by a member of the body).&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(2) Proceedings in England and Wales or Northern Ireland for such an offence must be brought against the body in its own name.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(3) For the purposes of proceedings for such an offence brought against an unincorporated body—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-45-3-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">rules of court relating to the service of documents have effect as if the body were a body corporate;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-45-3-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">the following provisions apply as they apply in relation to a body corporate—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4No" id="regulation-45-3-b-i">(i)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4Text">section 33 (procedure on charge of offence against corporation) of the Criminal Justice Act 1925(<a class="LegFootnoteRef" href="#f00049" title="Go to footnote 49" id="Backf00049">49</a>) and Schedule 3 (Corporations) to the Magistrates' Courts Act 1980(<a class="LegFootnoteRef" href="#f00050" title="Go to footnote 50" id="Backf00050">50</a>);</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4No" id="regulation-45-3-b-ii">(ii)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4Text">section 18 (procedure on charge) of the Criminal Justice Act (Northern Ireland) 1945(<a class="LegFootnoteRef" href="#f00051" title="Go to footnote 51" id="Backf00051">51</a>) and Article 166 (corporations) of, and Schedule 4 (Corporations) to, the Magistrates' Courts (Northern Ireland) Order 1981(<a class="LegFootnoteRef" href="#f00052" title="Go to footnote 52" id="Backf00052">52</a>).</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(4) A fine imposed on an unincorporated body on its conviction of an offence under these Regulations is to be paid out of the funds of the body.&#xD;</p><div class="LegAnnotations"><p class="LegAnnotationsGroupHeading">Commencement Information</p><div class="LegCommentaryItem" id="commentary-key-4b3383442f56ba912a015e3ca21c6d46"><p class="LegCommentaryPara"><span class="LegCommentaryType">I45</span><span class="LegCommentaryText">Reg. 45 in force at 15.10.2026, see <a class="LegCitation" href="/id/uksi/2026/1071/regulation/1/2" title="Go to reg. 1(2)" rel="cite"><strong>reg. 1(2)</strong></a></span></p></div></div><h3 class="LegP1GroupTitle">Time limit for proceedings for summary offences<span class="LegExtentRestriction" title="Applies to "><span class="btr"></span>U.K.<span class="bbl"></span><span class="bbr"></span></span></h3><p class="LegP1ParaText"><span class="LegP1No" id="regulation-46">46.</span>—(1) Proceedings for an offence under these Regulations which is triable only summarily may be brought within the period of 12 months beginning with the date on which evidence sufficient in the opinion of the prosecutor to justify the proceedings comes to the prosecutor's knowledge.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(2) But such proceedings may not be brought by virtue of <a href="#regulation-46-1" title="Go to reg. 46(1)">paragraph (1)</a> more than 3 years after the commission of the offence.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(3) A certificate signed by the prosecutor as to the date on which the evidence in question came to the prosecutor's knowledge is conclusive evidence of the date on which it did so; and a certificate to that effect and purporting to be so signed is to be treated as being so signed unless the contrary is proved.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(4) In relation to proceedings in Scotland—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-46-4-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text"><a class="LegCitation" title="Go to item of legislation" rel="cite" href="https://www.legislation.gov.uk/ukpga/1995/46/section/136">section 136</a> <a class="LegCitation" title="Go to item of legislation" rel="cite" href="https://www.legislation.gov.uk/ukpga/1995/46/section/136">(3)</a> (date of commencement of summary proceedings) of the <a class="LegCitation" title="Go to item of legislation" rel="cite" href="https://www.legislation.gov.uk/ukpga/1995/46/contents">Criminal Procedure (Scotland) Act 1995</a>(<a class="LegFootnoteRef" href="#f00053" title="Go to footnote 53" id="Backf00053">53</a>) applies for the purposes of <a href="#regulation-46" title="Go to reg. 46">this regulation</a> as it applies for the purposes of <a class="LegCitation" title="Go to item of legislation" rel="cite" href="https://www.legislation.gov.uk/ukpga/1995/46/section/136">that section</a>, and</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-46-4-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">references in <a href="#regulation-46" title="Go to reg. 46">this regulation</a> to the prosecutor are to be treated as references to the Lord Advocate.</span>&#xD;</p><div class="LegAnnotations"><p class="LegAnnotationsGroupHeading">Commencement Information</p><div class="LegCommentaryItem" id="commentary-key-452f94cdfbd4d32ed71487ce74ef9e7d"><p class="LegCommentaryPara"><span class="LegCommentaryType">I46</span><span class="LegCommentaryText">Reg. 46 in force at 15.10.2026, see <a class="LegCitation" href="/id/uksi/2026/1071/regulation/1/2" title="Go to reg. 1(2)" rel="cite"><strong>reg. 1(2)</strong></a></span></p></div></div><h3 class="LegP1GroupTitle">Application of Chapter 1 of Part 2 of the Serious Organised Crime and Police Act 2005<span class="LegExtentRestriction" title="Applies to "><span class="btr"></span>U.K.<span class="bbl"></span><span class="bbr"></span></span></h3><p class="LegP1ParaText"><span class="LegP1No" id="regulation-47">47.</span>  Chapter 1 of Part 2 (Investigatory powers) of the Serious Organised Crime and Police Act 2005(<a class="LegFootnoteRef" href="#f00054" title="Go to footnote 54" id="Backf00054">54</a>) applies to any offence under <a href="#part-3" title="Go to Pt. 3">Part 3</a> (Finance) or <a href="#regulation-30" title="Go to reg. 30">regulation 30</a> (finance: licensing offences).&#xD;</p><div class="LegAnnotations"><p class="LegAnnotationsGroupHeading">Commencement Information</p><div class="LegCommentaryItem" id="commentary-key-a3e65e3cbc483921d382fd573811fcd9"><p class="LegCommentaryPara"><span class="LegCommentaryType">I47</span><span class="LegCommentaryText">Reg. 47 in force at 15.10.2026, see <a class="LegCitation" href="/id/uksi/2026/1071/regulation/1/2" title="Go to reg. 1(2)" rel="cite"><strong>reg. 1(2)</strong></a></span></p></div></div><a class="LegAnchorID" id="part-9"></a><div class="LegClearPart"></div><h2 class="LegPart"><span class="LegPartNo">Part 9<span class="LegExtentRestriction" title="Applies to "><span class="btr"></span>U.K.<span class="bbl"></span><span class="bbr"></span></span></span><span class="LegPartTitle">Revocation and transitional provision</span></h2><h3 class="LegP1GroupTitleFirst">Revocation of the Global Human Rights Sanctions Regulations<span class="LegExtentRestriction" title="Applies to "><span class="btr"></span>U.K.<span class="bbl"></span><span class="bbr"></span></span></h3><p class="LegP1ParaText"><span class="LegP1No" id="regulation-48">48.</span>  The Global Human Rights Sanctions Regulations 2020(<a class="LegFootnoteRef" href="#f00055" title="Go to footnote 55" id="Backf00055">55</a>) are revoked.&#xD;</p><div class="LegAnnotations"><p class="LegAnnotationsGroupHeading">Commencement Information</p><div class="LegCommentaryItem" id="commentary-key-8d34a97efebc34d780824216e1f26b91"><p class="LegCommentaryPara"><span class="LegCommentaryType">I48</span><span class="LegCommentaryText">Reg. 48 in force at 15.10.2026, see <a class="LegCitation" href="/id/uksi/2026/1071/regulation/1/2" title="Go to reg. 1(2)" rel="cite"><strong>reg. 1(2)</strong></a></span></p></div></div><h3 class="LegP1GroupTitle">Transitional provision: designations<span class="LegExtentRestriction" title="Applies to "><span class="btr"></span>U.K.<span class="bbl"></span><span class="bbr"></span></span></h3><p class="LegP1ParaText"><span class="LegP1No" id="regulation-49">49.</span>—(1) This regulation applies to a person (“<span class="LegTerm" id="term-p">P</span>”) who, immediately before the relevant date, was designated by the Secretary of State under regulation 5 (power to designate persons) of the 2020 Regulations for the purposes of—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-49-1-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">regulations 11 to 15 (asset-freeze etc.) of those Regulations,</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-49-1-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">regulation 16A (director disqualification) of those Regulations, or</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-49-1-c">(c)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">regulation 17 (immigration) of those Regulations.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(2) P is to be treated on and after the relevant date as if P had been designated by the Secretary of State under regulation 5(1) (power to designate persons by name) for the purposes of (as the case may be)—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-49-2-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">regulations 14 to 18 (asset-freeze etc.),</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-49-2-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">regulation 20 (director disqualification), or</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-49-2-c">(c)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">regulation 21 (immigration).</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(3) Any reference in a provision mentioned in paragraph (4) to the date on which P became a designated person is a reference to the date on which P was designated by the Secretary of State under regulation 5 of the 2020 Regulations.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(4) The provisions referred to in paragraph (3) are—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-49-4-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">regulation 6(6) (conditions for the designation of persons by name),</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-49-4-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">regulation 22(5) (finance: exceptions from prohibitions), and</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-49-4-c">(c)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">paragraph 8(a) of Schedule 2 (Treasury licences: purposes).</span>&#xD;</p><div class="LegAnnotations"><p class="LegAnnotationsGroupHeading">Commencement Information</p><div class="LegCommentaryItem" id="commentary-key-5e9672d88913d11f185245256de722b1"><p class="LegCommentaryPara"><span class="LegCommentaryType">I49</span><span class="LegCommentaryText">Reg. 49 in force at 15.10.2026, see <a class="LegCitation" href="/id/uksi/2026/1071/regulation/1/2" title="Go to reg. 1(2)" rel="cite"><strong>reg. 1(2)</strong></a></span></p></div></div><h3 class="LegP1GroupTitle">Transitional provision: Treasury licences<span class="LegExtentRestriction" title="Applies to "><span class="btr"></span>U.K.<span class="bbl"></span><span class="bbr"></span></span></h3><p class="LegP1ParaText"><span class="LegP1No" id="regulation-50">50.</span>—(1) <a href="#regulation-50-2" title="Go to reg. 50(2)">Paragraphs (2)</a> to <a href="#regulation-50-4" title="Go to reg. 50(4)">(4)</a> apply to a licence which—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-50-1-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">was issued by the Treasury under regulation 20(1) (Treasury licences) of the 2020 Regulations,</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-50-1-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">was in effect immediately before the relevant date, and</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-50-1-c">(c)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">authorises conduct which would on and after the relevant date, and in the absence of <a href="#regulation-50-2" title="Go to reg. 50(2)">paragraphs (2)</a> to <a href="#regulation-50-4" title="Go to reg. 50(4)">(4)</a> be prohibited under <a href="#part-3" title="Go to Pt. 3">Part 3</a> (Finance),</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2Text">and such a licence is referred to in <a href="#regulation-50" title="Go to reg. 50">this regulation</a> as an “<span class="LegTerm" id="term-existing-treasury-licence">existing Treasury licence</span>”.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(2) An existing Treasury licence has effect on and after the relevant date as if it had been issued by the Treasury under <a href="#regulation-26" title="Go to reg. 26">regulation 26</a><a href="#regulation-25-1" title="Go to reg. 25(1)">(1)</a> (Treasury licences).&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(3) Any reference in an existing Treasury licence to the 2020 Regulations is to be treated on and after the relevant date as a reference to these Regulations.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(4) Any reference in an existing Treasury licence to a prohibition in Part 3 (Finance) of the 2020 Regulations is to be treated on and after the relevant date as a reference to the corresponding prohibition in Part 3.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(5) <a href="#regulation-50-6" title="Go to reg. 50(6)">Paragraph (6)</a> applies where—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-50-5-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">an application for a licence, or for the variation of a licence, under the 2020 Regulations was made before the relevant date,</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-50-5-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">the application is for the authorisation of conduct which would (on or after the relevant date) be prohibited under <a href="#part-3" title="Go to Pt. 3">Part 3</a>, and</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-50-5-c">(c)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">a decision to grant or refuse the application has not been made before that date.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(6) The application is to be treated on and after the relevant date as an application for a licence, or for the variation of a licence (as the case may be), under <a href="#regulation-26" title="Go to reg. 26">regulation 26</a><a href="#regulation-25-1" title="Go to reg. 25(1)">(1)</a>.&#xD;</p><div class="LegAnnotations"><p rel="cite"><strong>reg. 1(2)</strong></a></span></p></div></div><h3 class="LegP1GroupTitle">Transitional provision: director disqualification licences<span class="LegExtentRestriction" title="Applies to "><span class="btr"></span>U.K.<span class="bbl"></span><span class="bbr"></span></span></h3><p class="LegP1ParaText"><span class="LegP1No" id="regulation-51">51.</span>—(1) <a which—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-51-1-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">was issued by the Secretary of State under regulation 20A (director disqualification licences) of the 2020 Regulations,</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" title="Go to reg. 51(2)">paragraphs (2)</a> to <a href="#regulation-51-4" title="Go to reg. 51(4)">(4)</a>) be prohibited under <a href="#part-4" title="Go to Pt. 4">Part 4</a> (Director licences).&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(3) Any reference in an existing director disqualification licence to the 2020 Regulations is to be treated on and after the relevant date as a reference to these Regulations.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(4) Any reference in an existing director disqualification licence to a prohibition in Part 3A (Director disqualification sanctions) of the 2020 Regulations is to be treated on and after the relevant date as a reference to the corresponding prohibition in Part 4.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(5) <a href="#regulation-51-6" title="Go to reg. 51(6)">Paragraph (6)</a> applies where—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-51-5-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">an application for a licence, or for a variation of a licence, under the 2020 Regulations was made before the relevant date,</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-51-5-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">the application is for the authorisation of conduct which would (on and after the relevant date) be prohibited under <a href="#part-4" title="Go to Pt. 4">Part 4</a>, and</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-51-5-c">(c)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">a decision to grant or refuse the application has not been made before that date.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(6) The application is to be treated on and after the relevant date as an application for a licence, or for the variation of a licence (as the case may be), under <a href="#regulation-27" title="Go to reg. 27">regulation 27</a>.&#xD;</p><div class="LegAnnotations"><p class="LegCommentaryType">I51</span><span class="LegCommentaryText">Reg. 51 in force at 15.10.2026, see <a class="LegCitation" href="/id/uksi/2026/1071/regulation/1/2" title="Go to reg. 1(2)" rel="cite"><strong>reg. 1(2)</strong></a></span></p></div></div><h3 class="LegP1GroupTitle">Interpretation of <a href="#part-9" title="Go to Pt. 9">Part 9</a><span class="LegExtentRestriction" title="Applies to "><span class="btr"></span>U.K.<span class="bbl"></span><span class="bbr"></span></span></h3><p class="LegP1ParaText"><span class="LegP1No" id="regulation-52">52.</span>  In <a href="#part-9" title="Go to Pt. 9">this Part</a>—&#xD;</p><a class="LegAnchorID" id="p03969"></a><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3">“<span class="LegTerm" id="term-the-2020-regulations">the 2020 Regulations</span>” means the Global Human Rights Sanctions Regulations 2020;&#xD;</p><a class="LegAnchorID" id="p03973"></a><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3">“<span class="LegTerm" id="term-relevant-date">relevant date</span>” means the date on which these Regulations come into force.&#xD;</p><div class="LegAnnotations"><p class="LegAnnotationsGroupHeading">Commencement Information</p><div class="LegCommentaryItem" id="commentary-key-22871e340fd5b9e24ade2495191ef7bc"><p class="LegCommentaryPara"><span class="LegCommentaryType">I52</span><span class="LegCommentaryText">Reg. 52 in force at 15.10.2026, see <a class="LegCitation" href="/id/uksi/2026/1071/regulation/1/2" title="Go to reg. 1(2)" rel="cite"><strong>reg. 1(2)</strong></a></span></p></div></div><div class="LegClearFix LegSignedSection"><div class="LegClearFix LegSignatory"><div class="LegClearFix LegSignee"><p class="LegPersonName">Stephen Doughty</p><p class="LegJobTitle">Minister of State</p><p class="LegDepartment">Foreign, Commonwealth and Development Office</p><p class="LegDateSigned">12th October 2026</p></div></div></div><h1 class="LegSchedulesTitle">SCHEDULES</h1><a class="LegAnchorID" id="schedule-1"></a><p class="LegArticleRef">Regulation 9(3)</p><h2 class="LegScheduleFirst"><span class="LegScheduleNo LegHeadingRef">Schedule 1<span class="LegExtentRestriction" title="Applies to "><span class="btr"></span>U.K.<span class="bbl"></span><span class="bbr"></span></span></span><span class="LegTitleBlockTitle">Rules for interpretation of regulation 9(2)</span></h2><h3 class="LegP1GroupTitle">Application of Schedule<span class="LegExtentRestriction" title="Applies to "><span class="btr"></span>U.K.<span class="bbl"></span><span class="bbr"></span></span></h3><p class="LegP1ParaText"><span class="LegP1No" id="schedule-1-paragraph-1">1.</span>—(1) The rules set out in the following paragraphs of this Schedule apply for the purpose of interpreting regulation 9(2) (meaning of “<span class="LegTerm" id="term-owned-or-controlled-directly-or-indirectly">owned or controlled directly or indirectly</span>”).&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(2) They also apply for the purpose of interpreting this Schedule.&#xD;</p><div class="LegAnnotations"><p class="LegAnnotationsGroupHeading">Commencement Information</p><div class="LegCommentaryItem" id="commentary-key-78647ffa1c074fad504d9fd6866805a7"><p class="LegCommentaryPara"><span class="LegCommentaryType">I53</span><span class="LegCommentaryText">Sch. 1 para. 1 in force at 15.10.2026, see <a class="LegCitation" href="/id/uksi/2026/1071/regulation/1/2" title="Go to reg. 1(2)" rel="cite"><strong>reg. 1(2)</strong></a></span></p></div></div><h3 class="LegP1GroupTitle">Joint interests<span class="LegExtentRestriction" title="Applies to "><span class="btr"></span>U.K.<span class="bbl"></span><span class="bbr"></span></span></h3><p class="LegP1ParaText"><span class="LegP1No" id="schedule-1-paragraph-2">2.</span>  If two or more persons each hold a share or right jointly, each of them is treated as holding that share or right.&#xD;</p><div class="LegAnnotations"><p class="LegAnnotationsGroupHeading">Commencement Information</p><div class="LegCommentaryItem" id="commentary-key-fbf7aafe0bfe1c87b4867aa7eaf6adbf"><p class="LegCommentaryPara"><span class="LegCommentaryType">I54</span><span class="LegCommentaryText">Sch. 1 para. 2 in force at 15.10.2026, see <a class="LegCitation" href="/id/uksi/2026/1071/regulation/1/2" title="Go to reg. 1(2)" rel="cite"><strong>reg. 1(2)</strong></a></span></p></div></div><h3 class="LegP1GroupTitle">Joint arrangements<span class="LegExtentRestriction" title="Applies to "><span class="btr"></span>U.K.<span class="bbl"></span><span class="bbr"></span></span></h3><p class="LegP1ParaText"><span class="LegP1No" id="schedule-1-paragraph-3">3.</span>—(1) If shares or rights held by a person and shares or rights held by another person are the subject of a joint arrangement between those persons, each of them is treated as holding the combined shares or rights of both of them.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(2) A “joint arrangement” is an arrangement between the holders of shares or rights that they will exercise all or substantially all the rights conferred by their respective shares or rights jointly in a way that is pre-determined by the arrangement.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(3) “<span class="LegTerm" id="term-arrangement">Arrangement</span>” has the meaning given by paragraph 12 of this Schedule.&#xD;</p><div class="LegAnnotations"><p class="LegAnnotationsGroupHeading">Commencement Information</p><div class="LegCommentaryItem" id="commentary-key-c94cc81884385d8c870355383b42ddbc"><p class="LegCommentaryPara"><span class="LegCommentaryType">I55</span><span class="LegCommentaryText">Sch. 1 para. 3 in force at 15.10.2026, see <a class="LegCitation" href="/id/uksi/2026/1071/regulation/1/2" title="Go to reg. 1(2)" rel="cite"><strong>reg. 1(2)</strong></a></span></p></div></div><h3 class="LegP1GroupTitle">Calculating shareholdings<span class="LegExtentRestriction" title="Applies to "><span class="btr"></span>U.K.<span class="bbl"></span><span class="bbr"></span></span></h3><p class="LegP1ParaText"><span class="LegP1No" id="schedule-1-paragraph-4">4.</span>—(1) In relation to a person who has a share capital, a reference to holding “more than 50% of the shares” in that person is to holding shares comprised in the issued share capital of that person of a nominal value exceeding (in aggregate) 50% of that share capital.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(2) In relation to a person who does not have a share capital—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="schedule-1-paragraph-4-2-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">a reference to holding shares in that person is to holding a right or rights to share in the capital or, as the case may be, profits of that person;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="schedule-1-paragraph-4-2-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">a reference to holding “more than 50% of the shares” in that person is to holding a right or rights to share in more than 50% of the capital or, as the case may be, profits of that person.</span>&#xD;</p><div class="LegAnnotations"><p class="LegAnnotationsGroupHeading">Commencement Information</p><div class="LegCommentaryItem" id="commentary-key-544e059e5cc0bc531d5cf95f635bf3a4"><p class="LegCommentaryPara"><span class="LegCommentaryType">I56</span><span class="LegCommentaryText">Sch. 1 para. 4 in force at 15.10.2026, see <a class="LegCitation" href="/id/uksi/2026/1071/regulation/1/2" title="Go to reg. 1(2)" rel="cite"><strong>reg. 1(2)</strong></a></span></p></div></div><h3 class="LegP1GroupTitle">Voting rights<span class="LegExtentRestriction" title="Applies to "><span class="btr"></span>U.K.<span class="bbl"></span><span class="bbr"></span></span></h3><p class="LegP1ParaText"><span class="LegP1No" id="schedule-1-paragraph-5">5.</span>—(1) A reference to the voting rights in a person is to the rights conferred on shareholders in respect of their shares (or, in the case of a person not having a share capital, on members) to vote at general meetings of the person on all or substantially all matters.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(2) In relation to a person that does not have general meetings at which matters are decided by the exercise of voting rights—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="schedule-1-paragraph-5-2-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">a reference to holding voting rights in the person is to be read as a reference to holding rights in relation to the person that are equivalent to those of a person entitled to exercise voting rights in a company;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="schedule-1-paragraph-5-2-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">a reference to holding “more than 50% of the voting rights” in the person is to be read as a reference to holding the right under the constitution of the person to block changes to the overall policy of the person or to the terms of its constitution.</span>&#xD;</p><div class="LegAnnotations"><p class="LegAnnotationsGroupHeading">Commencement Information</p><div class="LegCommentaryItem" id="commentary-key-50f3b4305211a787fba646d8d5ef23a5"><p class="LegCommentaryPara"><span class="LegCommentaryType">I57</span><span class="LegCommentaryText">Sch. 1 para. 5 in force at 15.10.2026, see <a class="LegCitation" href="/id/uksi/2026/1071/regulation/1/2" title="Go to reg. 1(2)" rel="cite"><strong>reg. 1(2)</strong></a></span></p></div></div><p class="LegP1ParaText LegExtentContainer" id="schedule-1-paragraph-6"><span class="LegP1No" id="schedule-1-paragraph-6">6.</span>  In applying regulation 9(2) (meaning of “<span class="LegTerm" id="term-owned-or-controlled-directly-or-indirectly">owned or controlled directly or indirectly</span>”) and this Schedule, the voting rights in a person are to be reduced by any rights held by the person itself.<span class="LegExtentRestriction" title="Applies to "><span class="btr"></span>U.K.<span class="bbl"></span><span class="bbr"></span></span></p><div class="LegAnnotations"><p class="LegAnnotationsGroupHeading">Commencement Information</p><div class="LegCommentaryItem" id="commentary-key-6a837e06f4fcca0403a5ae40a658b7c0"><p class="LegCommentaryPara"><span class="LegCommentaryType">I58</span><span class="LegCommentaryText">Sch. 1 para. 6 in force at 15.10.2026, see <a class="LegCitation" href="/id/uksi/2026/1071/regulation/1/2" title="Go to reg. 1(2)" rel="cite"><strong>reg. 1(2)</strong></a></span></p></div></div><h3 class="LegP1GroupTitle">Rights to appoint or remove members of the board<span class="LegExtentRestriction" title="Applies to "><span class="btr"></span>U.K.<span class="bbl"></span><span class="bbr"></span></span></h3><p class="LegP1ParaText"><span class="LegP1No" id="schedule-1-paragraph-7">7.</span>  A reference to the right to appoint or remove a majority of the board of directors of a person is to the right to appoint or remove directors holding a majority of the voting rights at meetings of the board on all or substantially all matters.&#xD;</p><div class="LegAnnotations"><p class="LegAnnotationsGroupHeading">Commencement Information</p><div class="LegCommentaryItem" id="commentary-key-657236c9318924de4a8b7ab80b50f55c"><p class="LegCommentaryPara"><span class="LegCommentaryType">I59</span><span class="LegCommentaryText">Sch. 1 para. 7 in force at 15.10.2026, see <a class="LegCitation" href="/id/uksi/2026/1071/regulation/1/2" title="Go to reg. 1(2)" rel="cite"><strong>reg. 1(2)</strong></a></span></p></div></div><p class="LegP1ParaText"><span class="LegP1No" id="schedule-1-paragraph-8">8.</span>  A reference to a board of directors, in the case of a person who does not have such a board, is to be read as a reference to the equivalent management body of that person.&#xD;</p><div class="LegAnnotations"><p class="LegAnnotationsGroupHeading">Commencement Information</p><div class="LegCommentaryItem" id="commentary-key-81805ba92416764604ffe2a52445e140"><p class="LegCommentaryPara"><span class="LegCommentaryType">I60</span><span class="LegCommentaryText">Sch. 1 para. 8 in force at 15.10.2026, see <a class="LegCitation" href="/id/uksi/2026/1071/regulation/1/2" title="Go to reg. 1(2)" rel="cite"><strong>reg. 1(2)</strong></a></span></p></div></div><h3 class="LegP1GroupTitle">Shares or rights held “indirectly”<span class="LegExtentRestriction" title="Applies to "><span class="btr"></span>U.K.<span class="bbl"></span><span class="bbr"></span></span></h3><p class="LegP1ParaText"><span class="LegP1No" id="schedule-1-paragraph-9">9.</span>—(1) A person holds a share “indirectly” if the person has a majority stake in another person and that other person—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="schedule-1-paragraph-9-1-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">holds the share in question, or</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="schedule-1-paragraph-9-1-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">is part of a chain of persons—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4No" id="schedule-1-paragraph-9-1-b-i">(i)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4Text">each of whom (other than the last) has a majority stake in the person immediately below it in the chain, and</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4No" id="schedule-1-paragraph-9-1-b-ii">(ii)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4Text">the last of whom holds the share.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(2) A person holds a right “indirectly” if the person has a majority stake in another person and that other person—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="schedule-1-paragraph-9-2-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">holds that right, or</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="schedule-1-paragraph-9-2-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">is part of a chain of persons—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4No" id="schedule-1-paragraph-9-2-b-i">(i)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4Text">each of whom (other than the last) has a majority stake in the person immediately below it in the chain, and</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4No" id="schedule-1-paragraph-9-2-b-ii">(ii)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4Text">the last of whom holds that right.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(3) For these purposes, a person (“<span class="LegTerm" id="term-a">A</span>”) has a “majority stake” in another person (“<span class="LegTerm" id="term-b">B</span>”) if—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="schedule-1-paragraph-9-3-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">A holds a majority of the voting rights in B,</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="schedule-1-paragraph-9-3-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">A is a member of B and has the right to appoint or remove a majority of the board of directors of B,</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="schedule-1-paragraph-9-3-c">(c)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">A is a member of B and controls alone, pursuant to an agreement with other shareholders or members, a majority of the voting rights in B, or</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="schedule-1-paragraph-9-3-d">(d)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">A has the right to exercise, or actually exercises, dominant influence or control over B.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(4) In the application of this paragraph to the right to appoint or remove a majority of the board of directors, a person (“<span class="LegTerm" id="term-a">A</span>”) is to be treated as having the right to appoint a director if—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="schedule-1-paragraph-9-4-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">any person's appointment as director follows necessarily from that person's appointment as director of A, or</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="schedule-1-paragraph-9-4-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">the directorship is held by A itself.</span>&#xD;</p><div class="LegAnnotations"><p class="LegAnnotationsGroupHeading">Commencement Information</p><div class="LegCommentaryItem" id="commentary-key-1f7f3136cd675f3748ca36d100a238b4"><p class="LegCommentaryPara"><span class="LegCommentaryType">I61</span><span class="LegCommentaryText">Sch. 1 para. 9 in force at 15.10.2026, see <a class="LegCitation" href="/id/uksi/2026/1071/regulation/1/2" title="Go to reg. 1(2)" rel="cite"><strong>reg. 1(2)</strong></a></span></p></div></div><h3 class="LegP1GroupTitle">Shares held by nominees<span class="LegExtentRestriction" title="Applies to "><span class="btr"></span>U.K.<span class="bbl"></span><span class="bbr"></span></span></h3><p class="LegP1ParaText"><span class="LegP1No" id="schedule-1-paragraph-10">10.</span>  A share held by a person as nominee for another is to be treated as held by the other (and not by the nominee).&#xD;</p><div class="LegAnnotations"><p class="LegAnnotationsGroupHeading">Commencement Information</p><div class="LegCommentaryItem" id="commentary-key-330215d80f45fddaf4af197269f8fde4"><p class="LegCommentaryPara"><span class="LegCommentaryType">I62</span><span class="LegCommentaryText">Sch. 1 para. 10 in force at 15.10.2026, see <a class="LegCitation" href="/id/uksi/2026/1071/regulation/1/2" title="Go to reg. 1(2)" rel="cite"><strong>reg. 1(2)</strong></a></span></p></div></div><h3 class="LegP1GroupTitle">Rights treated as held by person who controls their exercise<span class="LegExtentRestriction" title="Applies to "><span class="btr"></span>U.K.<span class="bbl"></span><span class="bbr"></span></span></h3><p class="LegP1ParaText"><span class="LegP1No" id="schedule-1-paragraph-11">11.</span>—(1) Where a person controls a right, the right is to be treated as held by that person (and not by the person who in fact holds the right, unless that person also controls it).&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(2) A person “controls” a right if, by virtue of any arrangement between that person and others, the right is exercisable only—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="schedule-1-paragraph-11-2-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">by that person,</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="schedule-1-paragraph-11-2-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">in accordance with that person's directions or instructions, or</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="schedule-1-paragraph-11-2-c">(c)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">with that person's consent or concurrence.</span>&#xD;</p><div class="LegAnnotations"><p class="LegAnnotationsGroupHeading">Commencement Information</p><div class="LegCommentaryItem" id="commentary-key-2c6c5d45934a490547ede5f79fb58e94"><p class="LegCommentaryPara"><span class="LegCommentaryType">I63</span><span class="LegCommentaryText">Sch. 1 para. 11 in force at 15.10.2026, see <a class="LegCitation" href="/id/uksi/2026/1071/regulation/1/2" title="Go to reg. 1(2)" rel="cite"><strong>reg. 1(2)</strong></a></span></p></div></div><p class="LegP1ParaText"><span class="LegP1No" id="schedule-1-paragraph-12">12.</span>  “<span class="LegTerm" id="term-arrangement">Arrangement</span>” includes—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="schedule-1-paragraph-12-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">any scheme, agreement or understanding, whether or not it is legally enforceable, and</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="schedule-1-paragraph-12-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">any convention, custom or practice of any kind.</span>&#xD;</p><div class="LegAnnotations"><p class="LegAnnotationsGroupHeading">Commencement Information</p><div class="LegCommentaryItem" id="commentary-key-0253333edfcc67cd7d7b73358ca94f75"><p class="LegCommentaryPara"><span class="LegCommentaryType">I64</span><span class="LegCommentaryText">Sch. 1 para. 12 in force at 15.10.2026, see <a class="LegCitation" href="/id/uksi/2026/1071/regulation/1/2" title="Go to reg. 1(2)" rel="cite"><strong>reg. 1(2)</strong></a></span></p></div></div><h3 class="LegP1GroupTitle">Rights exercisable only in certain circumstances etc.<span class="LegExtentRestriction" title="Applies to "><span class="btr"></span>U.K.<span class="bbl"></span><span class="bbr"></span></span></h3><p class="LegP1ParaText"><span class="LegP1No" id="schedule-1-paragraph-13">13.</span>—(1) Rights that are exercisable only in certain circumstances are to be taken into account only—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="schedule-1-paragraph-13-1-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">when the circumstances have arisen, and for so long as they continue to obtain, or</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="schedule-1-paragraph-13-1-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">when the circumstances are within the control of the person having the rights.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(2) But rights that are exercisable by an administrator or by creditors while a person is subject to relevant insolvency proceedings are not to be taken into account while the person is subject to those proceedings.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(3) “<span class="LegTerm" id="term-relevant-insolvency-proceedings">Relevant insolvency proceedings</span>” means—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="schedule-1-paragraph-13-3-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">administration within the meaning of the Insolvency Act 1986,</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="schedule-1-paragraph-13-3-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">administration within the meaning of the Insolvency (Northern Ireland) Order 1989, or</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="schedule-1-paragraph-13-3-c">(c)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">proceedings under the insolvency law of another country during which a person's assets and affairs are subject to the control or supervision of a third party or creditor.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(4) Rights that are normally exercisable but are temporarily incapable of exercise are to continue to be taken into account.&#xD;</p><div class="LegAnnotations"><p class="LegAnnotationsGroupHeading">Commencement Information</p><div class="LegCommentaryItem" id="commentary-key-ea8880f78e637fee8a3fb57be42fdceb"><p class="LegCommentaryPara"><span class="LegCommentaryType">I65</span><span class="LegCommentaryText">Sch. 1 para. 13 in force at 15.10.2026, see <a class="LegCitation" href="/id/uksi/2026/1071/regulation/1/2" title="Go to reg. 1(2)" rel="cite"><strong>reg. 1(2)</strong></a></span></p></div></div><h3 class="LegP1GroupTitle">Rights attached to shares held by way of security<span class="LegExtentRestriction" title="Applies to "><span class="btr"></span>U.K.<span class="bbl"></span><span class="bbr"></span></span></h3><p class="LegP1ParaText"><span class="LegP1No" id="schedule-1-paragraph-14">14.</span>  Rights attached to shares held by way of security provided by a person are to be treated for the purposes of this Schedule as held by that person—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="schedule-1-paragraph-14-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">where, apart from the right to exercise them for the purpose of preserving the value of the security, or of realising it, the rights are exercisable only in accordance with that person's instructions, and</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="schedule-1-paragraph-14-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">where the shares are held in connection with the granting of loans as part of normal business activities and apart from the right to exercise them for the purpose of preserving the value of the security, or of realising it, the rights are exercisable only in that person's interests.</span>&#xD;</p><div class="LegAnnotations"><p class="LegAnnotationsGroupHeading">Commencement Information</p><div class="LegCommentaryItem" id="commentary-key-1a55937ac4c4b92d31853dea14edb3ad"><p class="LegCommentaryPara"><span class="LegCommentaryType">I66</span><span class="LegCommentaryText">Sch. 1 para. 14 in force at 15.10.2026, see <a class="LegCitation" href="/id/uksi/2026/1071/regulation/1/2" title="Go to reg. 1(2)" rel="cite"><strong>reg. 1(2)</strong></a></span></p></div></div><a class="LegAnchorID" id="schedule-2"></a><div class="LegClearSchedule"></div><p class="LegArticleRef">Regulation 26(2)</p><h2 class="LegSchedule"><span class="LegScheduleNo LegHeadingRef">Schedule 2<span class="LegExtentRestriction" title="Applies to "><span class="btr"></span>U.K.<span class="bbl"></span><span class="bbr"></span></span></span><span class="LegTitleBlockTitle">Treasury licences: purposes</span></h2><h3 class="LegP1GroupTitle">Interpretation<span class="LegExtentRestriction" title="Applies to "><span class="btr"></span>U.K.<span class="bbl"></span><span class="bbr"></span></span></h3><p class="LegP1ParaText"><span class="LegP1No" id="schedule-2-paragraph-1">1.</span>—(1) In <a href="#schedule-2" title="Go to Sch. 2">this Schedule</a>—&#xD;</p><a class="LegAnchorID" id="p04264"></a><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3">“<span class="LegTerm" id="term-designated-person">designated person</span>” has the same meaning as it has in <a href="#part-3" title="Go to Pt. 3">Part 3</a> (Finance);&#xD;</p><a class="LegAnchorID" id="p04269"></a><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3">“<span class="LegTerm" id="term-frozen-account">frozen account</span>” has the same meaning as in regulation 22 (finance: exceptions from prohibitions);&#xD;</p><a class="LegAnchorID" id="p04273"></a><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3">“<span class="LegTerm" id="term-frozen-funds-or-economic-resources">frozen funds or economic resources</span>” means funds or economic resources frozen by virtue of <a href="#regulation-14" title="Go to reg. 14">regulation 14</a> (asset-freeze in relation to designated persons).&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(2) For the purposes of this Schedule, references to a designated person are to be read as including a person (“<span class="LegTerm" id="term-c">C</span>”) who is owned or controlled directly or indirectly (within the meaning of regulation 9 (meaning of “<span class="LegTerm" id="term-owned-or-controlled-directly-or-indirectly">owned or controlled directly or indirectly</span>”)) by the designated person (“<span class="LegTerm" id="term-p">P</span>”).&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(3) When determining for the purposes of paragraph 8 when a person became a designated person, C is to be treated as having become a designated person at the same time as P.&#xD;</p><div class="LegAnnotations"><p class="LegAnnotationsGroupHeading">Commencement Information</p><div class="LegCommentaryItem" id="commentary-key-db30f20b6c059a1a1099a9d6a70d649a"><p class="LegCommentaryPara"><span class="LegCommentaryType">I67</span><span class="LegCommentaryText">Sch. 2 para. 1 in force at 15.10.2026, see <a class="LegCitation" href="/id/uksi/2026/1071/regulation/1/2" title="Go to reg. 1(2)" rel="cite"><strong>reg. 1(2)</strong></a></span></p></div></div><h3 class="LegP1GroupTitle">Basic needs<span class="LegExtentRestriction" title="Applies to "><span class="btr"></span>U.K.<span class="bbl"></span><span class="bbr"></span></span></h3><p class="LegP1ParaText"><span class="LegP1No" id="schedule-2-paragraph-2">2.</span>—(1) To enable the basic needs of a designated person, or (in the case of an individual) any dependent family member of such a person, to be met.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(2) In the case of an individual, in <a href="#schedule-2-paragraph-2-1" title="Go to Sch. 2 para. 2(1)">sub-paragraph (1)</a> “<span class="LegTerm" id="term-basic-needs">basic needs</span>” includes—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="schedule-2-paragraph-2-2-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">medical needs;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="schedule-2-paragraph-2-2-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">needs for—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4No" id="schedule-2-paragraph-2-2-b-i">(i)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4Text">food;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4No" id="schedule-2-paragraph-2-2-b-ii">(ii)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4Text">payment of insurance premiums;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4No" id="schedule-2-paragraph-2-2-b-iii">(iii)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4Text">payment of tax;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4No" id="schedule-2-paragraph-2-2-b-iv">(iv)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4Text">rent or mortgage payments;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4No" id="schedule-2-paragraph-2-2-b-v">(v)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4Text">utility payments.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(3) In the case of a person other than an individual, in <a href="#schedule-2-paragraph-2-1" title="Go to Sch. 2 para. 2(1)">sub-paragraph (1)</a> “<span class="LegTerm" id="term-basic-needs">basic needs</span>” includes needs for—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="schedule-2-paragraph-2-3-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">payment of insurance premiums;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="schedule-2-paragraph-2-3-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">payment of reasonable fees for the provision of property management services;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="schedule-2-paragraph-2-3-c">(c)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">payment of remuneration, allowances or pensions of employees;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="schedule-2-paragraph-2-3-d">(d)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">payment of tax;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="schedule-2-paragraph-2-3-e">(e)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">rent or mortgage payments;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="schedule-2-paragraph-2-3-f">(f)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">utility payments.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(4) In <a href="#schedule-2-paragraph-2-1" title="Go to Sch. 2 para. 2(1)">sub-paragraph (1)</a>—&#xD;</p><a class="LegAnchorID" id="p04360"></a><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3">“<span class="LegTerm" id="term-dependent">dependent</span>” means financially dependent;&#xD;</p><a class="LegAnchorID" id="p04364"></a><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3">“<span class="LegTerm" id="term-family-member">family member</span>” includes—&#xD;</p><div><div class="LegAlphaList"><div class="LegListItem" id="p04367"><div class="LegLevel4No LegListItemNo">(a)</div><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel4">the wife or husband of the designated person;&#xD;</p></div><div class="LegListItem" id="p04371"><div class="LegLevel4No LegListItemNo">(b)</div><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel4">the civil partner of the designated person;&#xD;</p></div><div class="LegListItem" id="p04375"><div class="LegLevel4No LegListItemNo">(c)</div><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel4">any parent or other ascendant of the designated person;&#xD;</p></div><div class="LegListItem" id="p04379"><div class="LegLevel4No LegListItemNo">(d)</div><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel4">any child or other descendant of the designated person;&#xD;</p></div><div class="LegListItem" id="p04383"><div class="LegLevel4No LegListItemNo">(e)</div><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel4">any person who is a brother or sister of the designated person, or a child or other descendant of such a person.&#xD;</p></div></div></div><div class="LegAnnotations"><p class="LegAnnotationsGroupHeading">Commencement Information</p><div class="LegCommentaryItem" id="commentary-key-f37ac2bf074b2b2b021f423a9ce78eee"><p class="LegCommentaryPara"><span class="LegCommentaryType">I68</span><span class="LegCommentaryText">Sch. 2 para. 2 in force at 15.10.2026, see id="commentary-key-0d327fb90afbe2ed2f6fb95e228fca6c"><p class="LegCommentaryPara"><span class="LegCommentaryType">I77</span><span class="LegCommentaryText">Sch. 2 para. 11 in force at 15.10.2026, see <a class="LegCitation" href="/id/uksi/2026/1071/regulation/1/2" title="Go to reg. 1(2)" rel="cite"><strong>reg. 1(2)</strong></a></span></p></div></div><a class="LegAnchorID" id="Legislation-ExNote"></a><h2 class="LegExpNoteTitle">Explanatory Note</h2><p class="LegCommentText">(This note is not part of the Regulations)&#xD;</p> <p class="LegExpNoteText">These Regulations are made under the Sanctions and Anti-Money Laundering Act <a class="LegCitation" title="Go to item of legislation" rel="cite" href="/id/ukpga/2018/13">2018 (c. 13)</a> to establish a sanctions regime for the purposes of: deterring, and providing accountability for: activities which, if carried out by or on behalf of a State, would amount to serious violations of certain human rights by that State; serious violations of international humanitarian law; and obstruction of the delivery or distribution of, or access to humanitarian assistance around the world.&#xD;</p>  <p class="LegExpNoteText">The Regulations revoke and replace the Global Human Rights Sanctions Regulations 2020 (<a class="LegCitation" title="Go to item of legislation" rel="cite" href="/id/uksi/2020/680">S.I. 2020/680</a>) (“<span class="LegTerm">the 2020 Regulations</span>”).&#xD;</p>  <p class="LegExpNoteText">The Regulations confer a power on the Secretary of State to designate persons who are, or have been, involved: in activities which, if carried out by or on behalf of a State, would amount to serious violations of certain human rights by that State; in serious violations of international humanitarian law; or in the obstruction of the delivery or distribution of, or access to, humanitarian assistance. Designated persons may be excluded from the United Kingdom, may be made subject to financial sanctions, including having their funds or economic resources frozen, and may be made subject to director disqualification sanctions.&#xD;</p>  <p class="LegExpNoteText">The Regulations provide for certain exceptions to this sanctions regime (for example to allow for frozen accounts to be credited with interest or other earnings and to allow acts done for the purpose of national security or the prevention of serious crime). The Regulations also confer powers on the Treasury to issue licences in respect of activities that would otherwise be prohibited under the asset freeze, etc. provisions in regulations 14 to 18. Schedule 2 sets out the purposes under which the Treasury will issue licences to particular persons. The Regulations also confer powers on the Secretary of State to issue licences in respect of activities that would otherwise be prohibited under the director disqualification sanctions imposed.&#xD;</p>  <p class="LegExpNoteText">The Regulations prescribe powers for the provision and sharing of information to enable the effective implementation and enforcement of the sanctions regime. The Regulations make it a criminal offence to contravene, or circumvent, any of the prohibitions in these Regulations and prescribe the mode of trial and penalties that apply to such offences.&#xD;</p>  <p class="LegExpNoteText">The Regulations also contain supplementary provision, including transitional provision to ensure that designations made and licences issued under the 2020 Regulations continue to have effect under these Regulations.&#xD;</p>  <p class="LegExpNoteText">A full impact assessment has not been produced for this instrument as no, or no significant, impact on the private, voluntary or public sector is foreseen. Instead a de minimis assessment has been prepared as this instrument is likely to entail some costs for businesses, but the net impact is estimated to be below £5 million per year.&#xD;</p> <div class="LegFootnotes"><div class="LegClearFix LegFootnotesContainer"><div class="LegClearFix LegFootnote" id="f00001"><div class="LegFootnoteNoContainer">(<span class="LegFootnoteNo">1</span>)</div><p class="LegText">The power to make regulations under Part 1 of the Sanctions and Anti-Money Laundering Act <a class="LegCitation" title="Go to item of legislation" rel="cite" href="/id/ukpga/2018/13">2018 (c. 13)</a> is conferred on an “appropriate Minister”. Section 1(9)(a) of that Act defines an “appropriate Minister” as including the Secretary of State.&#xD;</p></div><div class="LegClearFix LegFootnote" id="f00002"><div class="LegFootnoteNoContainer">(<span class="LegFootnoteNo">2</span>)</div><p class="LegText"><a class="LegCitation" title="Go to item of legislation" rel="cite" href="/id/ukpga/2018/13">2018 c. 13</a>. Section 17(5)(b)(i) (enforcement) is amended by the Sentencing Act <a class="LegCitation" title="Go to item of legislation" rel="cite" href="/id/ukpga/2020/17">2020 (c. 17)</a>, Schedule 24, paragraph 443(1). Sections 1, 11 and 16 are amended by the Economic Crime (Transparency and Enforcement) Act <a class="LegCitation" title="Go to item of legislation" rel="cite" href="/id/ukpga/2022/10">2022 (c. 10)</a>, sections 57, 58, 59 and 65. Section 3A and 15(3A) and (3B) were inserted by the Economic Crime and Corporate Transparency Act <a class="LegCitation" title="Go to item of legislation" rel="cite" href="/id/ukpga/2023/56">2023 (c. 56)</a>, section 35(3) and is in force only for the limited purpose of conferring power to make regulations.&#xD;</p></div><div class="LegClearFix LegFootnote" id="f00003"><div class="LegFootnoteNoContainer">(<span class="LegFootnoteNo">3</span>)</div><p class="LegText"><a class="LegCitation" title="Go to item of legislation" rel="cite" href="/id/ukpga/1986/46">1986 c. 46</a>. Section 11A was inserted the Economic Crime and Corporate Transparency Act 2023, section 36.&#xD;</p></div><div class="LegClearFix LegFootnote" id="f00004"><div class="LegFootnoteNoContainer">(<span class="LegFootnoteNo">4</span>)</div><p class="LegText"><a class="LegCitation" title="Go to item of legislation" rel="cite" href="/id/nisi/2002/3150">S.I. 2002/3150 (N.I. 4)</a>. They aim to make the Statutory Instrument accessible to readers who are not legally qualified and accompany any Statutory Instrument or Draft Statutory Instrument laid before Parliament from June 2004 onwards.</p></div></div><div class="help" id="moreResourcesTabHelp"><span class="icon"></span><div class="content"><a href="#" class="close"><img alt="Close" src="/images/chrome/closeIcon.gif" /></a><h3>More Resources</h3><p>Access essential accompanying documents and information for this legislation item from this tab. Dependent on the legislation item being viewed this may include:</p><ul><li>the original print PDF of the as enacted version that was used for the print copy</li><li>lists of changes made by and/or affecting this legislation item</li><li>confers power and blanket amendment details</li><li>all formats of all associated documents</li><li>correction slips</li><li>links to related legislation and further information resources</li></ul></div></div><div class="help" id="timelineHelp"><span class="icon"></span><div class="content"><a href="#" class="close"><img alt="Close" src="/images/chrome/closeIcon.gif" /></a><h3>Timeline of Changes</h3><p id="Revised_toc_1">This timeline shows the different points in time where a change occurred. The dates will coincide with the earliest date on which the change (e.g an insertion, a repeal or a substitution) that was applied came into force. The first date in the timeline will usually be the earliest date when the provision came into force. In some cases the first date is 01/02/1991 (or for Northern Ireland legislation 01/01/2006). This date is our basedate. No versions before this date are available. For further information see the Editorial Practice Guide and Glossary under Help.</p></div></div><div class="help" id="moreResourcesHelp"><span class="icon"></span><div class="content"><a href="#" class="close"><img alt="Close" src="/images/chrome/closeIcon.gif" /></a><h3>More Resources</h3><p>Use this menu to access essential accompanying documents and information for this legislation item.
